local Plrs = game:GetService("Players")
local RS = game:GetService("RunService")
local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local TPS = game:GetService("TeleportService")
local WS = workspace
local Light = game:GetService("Lighting")
local Http = game:GetService("HttpService")
local Rep = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local VIM = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
local TS = game:GetService("TweenService")
local MS = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")
local SG = game:GetService("StarterGui")

local P = Plrs.LocalPlayer
local Cam = WS.CurrentCamera
local PId = game.PlaceId
local PName = "Unknown"
pcall(function() PName = MS:GetProductInfo(PId).Name end)

-- Cleanup
for k in pairs(_G) do
    if type(k) == "string" and k:find("^NW_") then
        _G[k] = nil
    end
end

pcall(function()
    for _, v in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("Highlight") and v.Name:find("^NW_") then
            v:Destroy()
        end
    end
end)

-- State
local S = {Run = true, C = nil, H = nil, R = nil}

local function GC()
    S.C = P.Character
    S.H = S.C and S.C:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    S.R = S.C and S.C:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    return S.C, S.H, S.R
end

GC()



local function CleanChar(c)
    if not c then return end
    pcall(function()
        for _, child in pairs(c:GetDescendants()) do
            if child:IsA("Highlight") or child:IsA("BodyVelocity") or child:IsA("BodyGyro") or child:IsA("BodyPosition") or child:IsA("BodyAngularVelocity") or child:IsA("SelectionBox") or child:IsA("BoxHandleAdornment") then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
        local hrp = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            hrp.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
            hrp.Transparency = 1
            hrp.CanCollide = false
        end
        local head = c:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head then head.Transparency = 0 end
    end)
end

-- Immediate startup cleanup
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(0.1)
    CleanChar(P.Character)
end)

P.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
    S.C = c
    S.H = c:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
    S.R = c:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 10)
    
    -- Reset flags
    _G.NW_Aim = false; _G.NW_ESP = false; _G.NW_HBOn = false
    _G.NW_Fly = false; _G.NW_NC = false; _G.NW_AF = false
    
    pcall(function()
        Cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
        Cam.CameraSubject = S.H
    end)
    
    task.spawn(function()
        for i = 1, 5 do
            CleanChar(c)
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end)

-- Notification function (reduced spam - only shows ON notifications by default)
local function NT(t, m, d, forceShow)
    -- Skip OFF notifications unless forced
    if m == "OFF" and not forceShow then return end
    pcall(function()
        SG:SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "[NW] " .. tostring(t), Text = tostring(m), Duration = d or 2})
    end)
end

local function SW(t)
    local s = tick()
    while tick() - s < t and S.Run do
        RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
end

local function Dst(a, b)
    if a and b then
        return (a - b).Magnitude
    end
    return math.huge
end

local function CreateHighlight(parent, name, color)
    if not parent then return nil end
    -- Don't highlight local player's character (robust check)
    if parent == P.Character then return nil end
    if S.C and parent == S.C then return nil end
    if Plrs:GetPlayerFromCharacter(parent) == P then return nil end
    -- Extra check: if parent has same name as local player and is a character model
    if parent:IsA("Model") and parent.Name == P.Name then return nil end
    
    pcall(function()
        local old = parent:FindFirstChild(name)
        if old then old:Destroy() end
    end)
    local h = Instance.new("Highlight")
    h.Name = name
    h.FillColor = color or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    h.FillTransparency = 0.5
    h.OutlineTransparency = 0.3
    h.Parent = parent
    return h
end

-- Remove blur effects on load (aggressive)
local function RemoveBlur()
    pcall(function()
        -- Remove from Lighting
        for _, effect in pairs(Light:GetChildren()) do
            if effect:IsA("BlurEffect") or effect:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") or effect:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") then
                effect.Enabled = false
                effect:Destroy()
            end
        end
        -- Remove from Camera
        for _, effect in pairs(Cam:GetChildren()) do
            if effect:IsA("BlurEffect") or effect:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
                effect.Enabled = false
                effect:Destroy()
            end
        end
        -- Remove from CoreGui (Fluent UI blur)
        local cg = game:GetService("CoreGui")
        for _, gui in pairs(cg:GetDescendants()) do
            if gui:IsA("BlurEffect") or gui:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
                gui.Enabled = false
                pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        -- Remove from PlayerGui
        local pg = P:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if pg then
            for _, gui in pairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
                if gui:IsA("BlurEffect") or gui:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
                    gui.Enabled = false
                    pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end)
                end
            end
        end
        -- Remove from workspace
        for _, obj in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if obj:IsA("BlurEffect") or obj:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
                obj.Enabled = false
                pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function ToggleDynamicBackground(on)
    _G.NW_DynBG = on
    if not on then
        if _G.NW_BG_Gui then _G.NW_BG_Gui:Destroy() _G.NW_BG_Gui = nil end
        return
    end
    
    if _G.NW_BG_Gui then return end 
    
    task.spawn(function()
        local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        sg.Name = "NW_Background"
        pcall(function() sg.Parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
        if not sg.Parent then sg.Parent = P:WaitForChild("PlayerGui") end
        sg.DisplayOrder = -999
        sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        _G.NW_BG_Gui = sg
        
        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
        f.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        f.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        f.Parent = sg
        
        local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        g.Color = ColorSequence.new{
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255))
        }
        g.Rotation = 45
        g.Parent = f
        
        local t = 0
        while _G.NW_DynBG and S.Run and sg.Parent do
            t = t + 0.002
            local h1 = t % 1
            local h2 = (t + 0.5) % 1
            g.Color = ColorSequence.new{
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromHSV(h1, 0.7, 0.3)),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromHSV(h2, 0.7, 0.3))
            }
            RS.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end
        if sg then sg:Destroy() end
        _G.NW_BG_Gui = nil
    end)
end

-- Auto remove blur continuously for first few seconds
task.spawn(function()
    for i = 1, 10 do
        RemoveBlur()
        task.wait(0.5)
    end
end)

-- Clean highlights from local player (aggressive)
local function CleanLocalHighlights()
    pcall(function()
        local char = P.Character or S.C
        if char then
            for _, child in pairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if child:IsA("Highlight") or child:IsA("PointLight") or child:IsA("SpotLight") or child:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
                    child:Destroy()
                end
                -- Remove selection boxes (visible hitbox)
                if child:IsA("SelectionBox") or child:IsA("BoxHandleAdornment") or child:IsA("SelectionSphere") then
                    child:Destroy()
                end
                -- Remove any adornments
                if child:IsA("HandleAdornment") or child:IsA("SurfaceGui") then
                    if child.Name:find("NW_") or child.Name:find("ESP") or child.Name:find("Hitbox") then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
            -- Also check direct children
            for _, child in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("Highlight") or child:IsA("SelectionBox") or child:IsA("BoxHandleAdornment") then
                    child:Destroy()
                end
            end
            -- Reset HumanoidRootPart to normal
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                hrp.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
                hrp.Transparency = 1
                hrp.CanCollide = false
                -- Remove any adornments from HRP
                for _, a in pairs(hrp:GetChildren()) do
                    if a:IsA("SelectionBox") or a:IsA("BoxHandleAdornment") or a:IsA("Highlight") then
                        a:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
            -- Reset Head transparency
            local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if head then
                head.Transparency = 0
            end
        end
        -- Clean from all parts attached to player
        for _, part in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("Highlight") or part:IsA("SelectionBox") or part:IsA("BoxHandleAdornment") then
                if part.Parent then
                    local partParent = part.Parent
                    if partParent == P.Character or partParent == S.C then
                        part:Destroy()
                    end
                    if partParent.Parent and (partParent.Parent == P.Character or partParent.Parent == S.C) then
                        part:Destroy()
                    end
                    if partParent:IsA("Model") and partParent.Name == P.Name then
                        part:Destroy()
                    end
                    -- Check Adornee property
                    if part:IsA("SelectionBox") and part.Adornee then
                        if part.Adornee.Parent == P.Character or part.Adornee.Parent == S.C then
                            part:Destroy()
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

local function DetectGame()
    if GDB[PId] then return GDB[PId] end
    local l = PName:lower()
    for k, v in pairs(KW) do
        if l:find(k) then return v end
    end
    if l:find("obby") or l:find("parkour") then return "Obby" end
    if l:find("tycoon") then return "Tycoon" end
    if l:find("simulator") then return "Simulator" end
    return "Universal"
end

local CG = DetectGame()
print("[NW] " .. CG .. " | " .. PName)

-- CORE FUNCTIONS
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local C = {}

function C.Spd(v)
    GC()
    if S.H then
        S.H.WalkSpeed = v
    end
end

function C.Jmp(v)
    GC()
    if S.H then
        S.H.JumpPower = v
    end
end

function C.Fly(on, spd)
    _G.NW_Fly = on
    _G.NW_FS = spd or 100
    GC()
    if not S.R then return end
    
    pcall(function()
        local bg = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_BG")
        local bv = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_BV")
        if bg then bg:Destroy() end
        if bv then bv:Destroy() end
    end)
    
    if on then
        local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
        bg.Name = "NW_BG"
        bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
        bg.P = 1e6
        bg.Parent = S.R
        
        local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        bv.Name = "NW_BV"
        bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
        bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        bv.Parent = S.R
        
        task.spawn(function()
            while _G.NW_Fly and S.Run do
                GC()
                if not S.R then break end
                local b1 = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_BG")
                local b2 = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_BV")
                if b1 then b1.CFrame = Cam.CFrame end
                if b2 then
                    local d = Vector3.zero
                    if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) then d = d + Cam.CFrame.LookVector end
                    if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.S) then d = d - Cam.CFrame.LookVector end
                    if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) then d = d - Cam.CFrame.RightVector end
                    if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) then d = d + Cam.CFrame.RightVector end
                    if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then d = d + Vector3.yAxis end
                    if UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) then d = d - Vector3.yAxis end
                    b2.Velocity = d.Magnitude > 0 and d.Unit * _G.NW_FS or Vector3.zero
                end
                RS.RenderStepped:Wait()
            end
        end)
    end
    NT("Fly", on and "ON" or "OFF", 2)
end

function C.NC(on)
    _G.NW_NC = on
    NT("Noclip", on and "ON" or "OFF", 2)
end

RS.Stepped:Connect(function()
    if _G.NW_NC then
        GC()
        if S.C then
            for _, p in pairs(S.C:GetDescendants()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") then
                    p.CanCollide = false
                end
            end
        end
    end
end)

function C.IJ(on)
    _G.NW_IJ = on
    NT("InfJump", on and "ON" or "OFF", 2)
end

UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
    if _G.NW_IJ then
        GC()
        if S.H then
            S.H:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
        end
    end
end)

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- ADVANCED ESP SYSTEM (Pro Elite Quality)
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local ESP = {
    Enabled = false,
    Highlights = true,
    Boxes = false,
    Tracers = false,
    Names = true,
    Health = true,
    Distance = true,
    Skeleton = false,
    TeamCheck = true,
    TeamColor = true,
    ShowNPCs = false,
    MaxDistance = 1000,
    BoxColor = Color3.new(1, 0, 0),
    TracerColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1),
    TracerOrigin = "Bottom", -- Bottom, Center, Mouse
    Objects = {}
}

local function CreateESPBox(player)
    local box = Drawing.new("Square")
    box.Visible = false
    box.Color = ESP.BoxColor
    box.Thickness = 1
    box.Transparency = 1
    box.Filled = false
    return box
end

local function CreateESPTracer(player)
    local tracer = Drawing.new("Line")
    tracer.Visible = false
    tracer.Color = ESP.TracerColor
    tracer.Thickness = 1
    tracer.Transparency = 1
    return tracer
end

local function CreateESPName(player)
    local name = Drawing.new("Text")
    name.Visible = false
    name.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    name.Size = 14
    name.Center = true
    name.Outline = true
    name.OutlineColor = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
    return name
end

local function CreateESPHealth(player)
    local healthBar = Drawing.new("Square")
    healthBar.Visible = false
    healthBar.Color = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
    healthBar.Thickness = 1
    healthBar.Filled = true
    local healthBg = Drawing.new("Square")
    healthBg.Visible = false
    healthBg.Color = Color3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2)
    healthBg.Thickness = 1
    healthBg.Filled = true
    return healthBar, healthBg
end

local function CreateESPSkeleton(player)
    local bones = {}
    local connections = {
        {"Head", "UpperTorso"}, {"UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"},
        {"UpperTorso", "LeftUpperArm"}, {"LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm"}, {"LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand"},
        {"UpperTorso", "RightUpperArm"}, {"RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm"}, {"RightLowerArm", "RightHand"},
        {"LowerTorso", "LeftUpperLeg"}, {"LeftUpperLeg", "LeftLowerLeg"}, {"LeftLowerLeg", "LeftFoot"},
        {"LowerTorso", "RightUpperLeg"}, {"RightUpperLeg", "RightLowerLeg"}, {"RightLowerLeg", "RightFoot"},
        -- R6 fallback
        {"Head", "Torso"}, {"Torso", "Left Arm"}, {"Torso", "Right Arm"}, {"Torso", "Left Leg"}, {"Torso", "Right Leg"}
    }
    for i = 1, 20 do
        local line = Drawing.new("Line")
        line.Visible = false
        line.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        line.Thickness = 1
        table.insert(bones, line)
    end
    return bones, connections
end

local function GetPlayerColor(player)
    if ESP.TeamColor and player.Team then
        return player.TeamColor and player.TeamColor.Color or Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    end
    if ESP.TeamCheck then
        return (player.Team == P.Team) and Color3.new(0, 1, 0) or Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
    end
    return ESP.BoxColor
end

local function UpdateESP()
    for player, objects in pairs(ESP.Objects) do
        pcall(function()
            if not player or not player.Parent or not player.Character then
                -- Cleanup
                for _, obj in pairs(objects) do
                    if type(obj) == "table" then
                        for _, o in pairs(obj) do pcall(function() o:Remove() end) end
                    else
                        pcall(function() obj:Remove() end)
                    end
                end
                ESP.Objects[player] = nil
                return
            end
            
            local char = player.Character
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            
            if not hrp or not head or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then
                for _, obj in pairs(objects) do
                    if type(obj) == "table" then
                        for _, o in pairs(obj) do pcall(function() o.Visible = false end) end
                    else
                        pcall(function() obj.Visible = false end)
                    end
                end
                return
            end
            
            GC()
            if not S.R then return end
            
            local dist = (hrp.Position - S.R.Position).Magnitude
            if dist > ESP.MaxDistance then
                for _, obj in pairs(objects) do
                    if type(obj) == "table" then
                        for _, o in pairs(obj) do pcall(function() o.Visible = false end) end
                    else
                        pcall(function() obj.Visible = false end)
                    end
                end
                return
            end
            
            local screenPos, onScreen = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)
            local headPos = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0))
            local footPos = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
            
            local color = GetPlayerColor(player)
            
            if onScreen then
                local height = math.abs(headPos.Y - footPos.Y)
                local width = height / 2
                
                -- Box
                if objects.Box and ESP.Boxes then
                    objects.Box.Size = Vector2.new(width, height)
                    objects.Box.Position = Vector2.new(screenPos.X - width/2, headPos.Y)
                    objects.Box.Color = color
                    objects.Box.Visible = true
                elseif objects.Box then
                    objects.Box.Visible = false
                end
                
                -- Tracer
                if objects.Tracer and ESP.Tracers then
                    local origin
                    if ESP.TracerOrigin == "Bottom" then
                        origin = Vector2.new(Cam.ViewportSize.X / 2, Cam.ViewportSize.Y)
                    elseif ESP.TracerOrigin == "Center" then
                        origin = Vector2.new(Cam.ViewportSize.X / 2, Cam.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
                    else
                        origin = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
                    end
                    objects.Tracer.From = origin
                    objects.Tracer.To = Vector2.new(screenPos.X, footPos.Y)
                    objects.Tracer.Color = color
                    objects.Tracer.Visible = true
                elseif objects.Tracer then
                    objects.Tracer.Visible = false
                end
                
                -- Name
                if objects.Name and ESP.Names then
                    local txt = player.Name
                    if ESP.Distance then
                        txt = txt .. " [" .. math.floor(dist) .. "m]"
                    end
                    objects.Name.Text = txt
                    objects.Name.Position = Vector2.new(screenPos.X, headPos.Y - 18)
                    objects.Name.Color = color
                    objects.Name.Visible = true
                elseif objects.Name then
                    objects.Name.Visible = false
                end
                
                -- Health Bar
                if objects.HealthBar and objects.HealthBg and ESP.Health then
                    local healthPercent = hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth
                    local barHeight = height * healthPercent
                    objects.HealthBg.Size = Vector2.new(3, height)
                    objects.HealthBg.Position = Vector2.new(screenPos.X - width/2 - 6, headPos.Y)
                    objects.HealthBg.Visible = true
                    objects.HealthBar.Size = Vector2.new(3, barHeight)
                    objects.HealthBar.Position = Vector2.new(screenPos.X - width/2 - 6, headPos.Y + (height - barHeight))
                    objects.HealthBar.Color = Color3.new(1 - healthPercent, healthPercent, 0)
                    objects.HealthBar.Visible = true
                elseif objects.HealthBar then
                    objects.HealthBar.Visible = false
                    objects.HealthBg.Visible = false
                end
                
                -- Skeleton
                if objects.Bones and ESP.Skeleton then
                    local boneIndex = 1
                    for _, conn in pairs(objects.BoneConnections) do
                        local part1 = char:FindFirstChild(conn[1])
                        local part2 = char:FindFirstChild(conn[2])
                        if part1 and part2 and boneIndex <= #objects.Bones then
                            local p1, o1 = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part1.Position)
                            local p2, o2 = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part2.Position)
                            if o1 and o2 then
                                objects.Bones[boneIndex].From = Vector2.new(p1.X, p1.Y)
                                objects.Bones[boneIndex].To = Vector2.new(p2.X, p2.Y)
                                objects.Bones[boneIndex].Color = color
                                objects.Bones[boneIndex].Visible = true
                            else
                                objects.Bones[boneIndex].Visible = false
                            end
                            boneIndex = boneIndex + 1
                        end
                    end
                elseif objects.Bones then
                    for _, bone in pairs(objects.Bones) do bone.Visible = false end
                end
            else
                for _, obj in pairs(objects) do
                    if type(obj) == "table" then
                        for _, o in pairs(obj) do pcall(function() o.Visible = false end) end
                    else
                        pcall(function() obj.Visible = false end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

local function CreatePlayerESP(player)
    if player == P then return end
    if ESP.Objects[player] then return end
    
    local objects = {}
    objects.Box = CreateESPBox(player)
    objects.Tracer = CreateESPTracer(player)
    objects.Name = CreateESPName(player)
    objects.HealthBar, objects.HealthBg = CreateESPHealth(player)
    objects.Bones, objects.BoneConnections = CreateESPSkeleton(player)
    
    -- Highlight
    if player.Character then
        local color = GetPlayerColor(player)
        if not player.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_E") then
            CreateHighlight(player.Character, "NW_E", color)
        end
    end
    
    ESP.Objects[player] = objects
end

local function CleanupPlayerESP(player)
    if ESP.Objects[player] then
        for _, obj in pairs(ESP.Objects[player]) do
            if type(obj) == "table" then
                for _, o in pairs(obj) do pcall(function() o:Remove() end) end
            else
                pcall(function() obj:Remove() end)
            end
        end
        ESP.Objects[player] = nil
    end
    if player.Character then
        local h = player.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_E")
        if h then h:Destroy() end
    end
end

function C.ESP(on)
    _G.NW_ESP = on
    ESP.Enabled = on
    if not on then
        for player in pairs(ESP.Objects) do
            CleanupPlayerESP(player)
        end
        NT("ESP", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    -- Create ESP for existing players
    for _, player in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= P then
            CreatePlayerESP(player)
        end
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while ESP.Enabled and S.Run do
            -- Update highlights
            for _, player in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                if player ~= P and player.Character then
                    if ESP.Highlights and not player.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_E") then
                        local color = GetPlayerColor(player)
                        CreateHighlight(player.Character, "NW_E", color)
                    elseif not ESP.Highlights then
                        local h = player.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_E")
                        if h then h:Destroy() end
                    end
                end
            end
            UpdateESP()
            RS.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("ESP", "ON", 2)
end

-- ESP Settings Functions
function C.ESPHighlights(on) ESP.Highlights = on end
function C.ESPBoxes(on) ESP.Boxes = on end
function C.ESPTracers(on) ESP.Tracers = on end
function C.ESPNames(on) ESP.Names = on end
function C.ESPHealth(on) ESP.Health = on end
function C.ESPDistance(on) ESP.Distance = on end
function C.ESPSkeleton(on) ESP.Skeleton = on end
function C.ESPTeamCheck(on) ESP.TeamCheck = on end
function C.ESPTeamColor(on) ESP.TeamColor = on end
function C.ESPShowNPCs(on) ESP.ShowNPCs = on end
function C.ESPMaxDistance(val) ESP.MaxDistance = val or 1000 end
function C.ESPTracerOrigin(origin) ESP.TracerOrigin = origin or "Bottom" end

-- NPC ESP
function C.NPCESP(on)
    _G.NW_NPCESP = on
    if not on then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            pcall(function() if o.Name == "NW_NPC" then o:Destroy() end end)
        end
        NT("NPC ESP", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_NPCESP and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, model in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if model:IsA("Model") and model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
                        -- Skip local player explicitly
                        if model == P.Character then continue end
                        if S.C and model == S.C then continue end
                        if model.Name == P.Name then continue end
                        -- Skip all players
                        local isPlayer = false
                        for _, plr in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                            if plr.Character == model then isPlayer = true break end
                        end
                        if not isPlayer and not model:FindFirstChild("NW_NPC") then
                            CreateHighlight(model, "NW_NPC", Color3.new(1, 0.5, 0))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("NPC ESP", "ON", 2)
end

-- Item/Pickup ESP
function C.ItemESP(on)
    _G.NW_ItemESP = on
    if not on then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            pcall(function() if o.Name == "NW_Item" then o:Destroy() end end)
        end
        NT("Item ESP", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_ItemESP and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, item in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if item:IsA("Tool") or (item:IsA("Model") and item:FindFirstChild("Handle")) then
                        if not item:FindFirstChild("NW_Item") then
                            CreateHighlight(item, "NW_Item", Color3.new(0, 1, 1))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("Item ESP", "ON", 2)
end

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- ADVANCED UNIVERSAL AIMBOT SYSTEM (Da Hood / Zee Hood / FPS Style)
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local Aim = {
    Enabled = false,
    Key = Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2, -- Default right click
    KeyName = "MouseButton2",
    Mode = "Hold", -- Hold, Toggle
    FOV = 150,
    ShowFOV = true,
    Smoothness = 0,
    AimPart = "Head",
    TeamCheck = true,
    WallCheck = false,
    Prediction = 0,
    StickyAim = false,
    CurrentTarget = nil,
    FOVCircle = nil,
    Locked = false
}

-- Create FOV Circle
local function CreateFOVCircle()
    if Aim.FOVCircle then pcall(function() Aim.FOVCircle:Remove() end) end
    local circle = Drawing.new("Circle")
    circle.Visible = Aim.ShowFOV
    circle.Radius = Aim.FOV
    circle.Color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    circle.Thickness = 1
    circle.Transparency = 1
    circle.Filled = false
    Aim.FOVCircle = circle
    return circle
end

-- Get closest player in FOV
local function GetClosestInFOV()
    local closest, closestDist = nil, Aim.FOV
    local mousePos = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
    
    for _, player in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= P and player.Character then
            local char = player.Character
            local aimPart = char:FindFirstChild(Aim.AimPart) or char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            
            if aimPart and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                -- Team check
                if Aim.TeamCheck and player.Team == P.Team then continue end
                
                -- Get screen position
                local screenPos, onScreen = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(aimPart.Position)
                if not onScreen then continue end
                
                -- Wall check
                if Aim.WallCheck then
                    GC()
                    if S.R then
                        local ray = Ray.new(S.R.Position, (aimPart.Position - S.R.Position).Unit * 500)
                        local hit = WS:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray, {P.Character, char})
                        if hit then continue end
                    end
                end
                
                -- Calculate distance from mouse
                local dist = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - mousePos).Magnitude
                if dist < closestDist then
                    closest = player
                    closestDist = dist
                end
            end
        end
    end
    
    return closest
end

-- Aim at target
local function AimAt(player)
    if not player or not player.Character then return end
    local char = player.Character
    local aimPart = char:FindFirstChild(Aim.AimPart) or char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not aimPart then return end
    
    local targetPos = aimPart.Position
    
    -- Apply prediction
    if Aim.Prediction > 0 then
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp and hrp:IsA("BasePart") then
            targetPos = targetPos + (hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity * Aim.Prediction)
        end
    end
    
    -- Smooth or instant aim
    if Aim.Smoothness > 0 then
        local currentCF = Cam.CFrame
        local targetCF = CFrame.lookAt(currentCF.Position, targetPos)
        Cam.CFrame = currentCF:Lerp(targetCF, 1 / Aim.Smoothness)
    else
        Cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Cam.CFrame.Position, targetPos)
    end
end

-- Update FOV circle position
local function UpdateFOVCircle()
    if Aim.FOVCircle then
        local mousePos = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
        Aim.FOVCircle.Position = mousePos
        Aim.FOVCircle.Radius = Aim.FOV
        Aim.FOVCircle.Visible = Aim.ShowFOV and Aim.Enabled
    end
end

-- Main aimbot loop
local function StartAimbot()
    task.spawn(function()
        while Aim.Enabled and S.Run do
            UpdateFOVCircle()
            
            -- Check if local character exists
            GC()
            if not S.C or not S.R or not S.H or S.H.Health <= 0 then
                Aim.Locked = false
                Aim.CurrentTarget = nil
                RS.RenderStepped:Wait()
                continue
            end
            
            if Aim.Locked then
                if Aim.StickyAim and Aim.CurrentTarget then
                    -- Stick to current target
                    if Aim.CurrentTarget.Character and Aim.CurrentTarget.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
                        local hum = Aim.CurrentTarget.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if hum.Health > 0 then
                            AimAt(Aim.CurrentTarget)
                        else
                            Aim.CurrentTarget = nil
                        end
                    else
                        Aim.CurrentTarget = nil
                    end
                else
                    -- Get new target
                    local target = GetClosestInFOV()
                    if target then
                        Aim.CurrentTarget = target
                        AimAt(target)
                    end
                end
            end
            
            RS.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end
    end)
end

-- Input handling for aimbot
UIS.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gpe)
    if gpe then return end
    if not Aim.Enabled then return end
    
    local isKey = (input.UserInputType == Aim.Key) or (input.KeyCode == Aim.Key)
    if isKey then
        if Aim.Mode == "Hold" then
            Aim.Locked = true
        else -- Toggle
            Aim.Locked = not Aim.Locked
        end
    end
end)

UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(input, gpe)
    if not Aim.Enabled then return end
    
    local isKey = (input.UserInputType == Aim.Key) or (input.KeyCode == Aim.Key)
    if isKey and Aim.Mode == "Hold" then
        Aim.Locked = false
        Aim.CurrentTarget = nil
    end
end)

-- Aimbot control functions
function C.Aimbot(on)
    _G.NW_Aim = on
    Aim.Enabled = on
    if not on then
        Aim.Locked = false
        Aim.CurrentTarget = nil
        if Aim.FOVCircle then Aim.FOVCircle.Visible = false end
        NT("Aimbot", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    CreateFOVCircle()
    StartAimbot()
    NT("Aimbot", "ON - " .. Aim.KeyName, 2)
end

function C.AimFOV(val) Aim.FOV = val or 150 if Aim.FOVCircle then Aim.FOVCircle.Radius = val end end
function C.AimShowFOV(on) Aim.ShowFOV = on if Aim.FOVCircle then Aim.FOVCircle.Visible = on and Aim.Enabled end end
function C.AimSmoothness(val) Aim.Smoothness = val or 0 end
function C.AimPrediction(val) Aim.Prediction = val or 0 end
function C.AimTeamCheck(on) Aim.TeamCheck = on end
function C.AimWallCheck(on) Aim.WallCheck = on end
function C.AimStickyAim(on) Aim.StickyAim = on end
function C.AimMode(mode) Aim.Mode = mode or "Hold" end
function C.AimPart(part) Aim.AimPart = part or "Head" end

function C.AimKey(keyName)
    Aim.KeyName = keyName or "MouseButton2"
    if keyName == "MouseButton1" then
        Aim.Key = Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
    elseif keyName == "MouseButton2" then
        Aim.Key = Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2
    elseif keyName == "E" then
        Aim.Key = Enum.KeyCode.E
    elseif keyName == "Q" then
        Aim.Key = Enum.KeyCode.Q
    elseif keyName == "F" then
        Aim.Key = Enum.KeyCode.F
    elseif keyName == "C" then
        Aim.Key = Enum.KeyCode.C
    elseif keyName == "X" then
        Aim.Key = Enum.KeyCode.X
    elseif keyName == "LeftShift" then
        Aim.Key = Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift
    elseif keyName == "LeftAlt" then
        Aim.Key = Enum.KeyCode.LeftAlt
    else
        Aim.Key = Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2
    end
    NT("AimKey", keyName, 2)
end

-- Silent Aim (cursor stays still, bullets go to target)
local SilentAim = {
    Enabled = false,
    FOV = 150,
    AimPart = "Head",
    TeamCheck = true,
    Prediction = 0.165
}

function C.SilentAim(on)
    _G.NW_SilentAim = on
    SilentAim.Enabled = on
    NT("Silent Aim", on and "ON" or "OFF", 2)
end

function C.SilentAimFOV(val) SilentAim.FOV = val or 150 end
function C.SilentAimPart(part) SilentAim.AimPart = part or "Head" end
function C.SilentAimTeamCheck(on) SilentAim.TeamCheck = on end
function C.SilentAimPrediction(val) SilentAim.Prediction = val or 0.165 end

-- Hook for silent aim (works in games with raycast) - Only if executor supports it
local oldNamecall
pcall(function()
    if hookmetamethod and getnamecallmethod then
        oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
            local method = getnamecallmethod()
            local args = {...}
            
            if SilentAim.Enabled then
                if method == "FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList" or method == "FindPartOnRay" or method == "Raycast" then
                    local target = GetClosestInFOV()
                    if target and target.Character then
                        local aimPart = target.Character:FindFirstChild(SilentAim.AimPart) or target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                        if aimPart then
                            GC()
                            if S.R then
                                local origin = S.R.Position
                                local direction = (aimPart.Position - origin).Unit * 1000
                                if method == "Raycast" then
                                    args[2] = origin
                                    args[3] = direction
                                else
                                    args[1] = Ray.new(origin, direction)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            
            return oldNamecall(self, unpack(args))
        end)
    end
end)

function C.Hitbox(on, size)
    size = size or 10
    _G.NW_HBOn = on
    _G.NW_HBSize = size
    if not on then
        C.ResetHitbox()
        return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_HBOn and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= P and p.Character then
                        local hrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if hrp then
                            hrp.Size = Vector3.new(_G.NW_HBSize, _G.NW_HBSize, _G.NW_HBSize)
                            hrp.Transparency = 1
                            hrp.CanCollide = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("Hitbox", tostring(size), 2)
end

function C.ResetHitbox()
    _G.NW_HBOn = false
    pcall(function()
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= P and p.Character then
                local hrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then
                    hrp.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
                    hrp.Transparency = 1
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    NT("Hitbox", "Reset", 2)
end

function C.TPTool()
    pcall(function()
        local mouse = P:GetMouse()
        mouse.Button1Down:Connect(function()
            if _G.NW_TPT then
                GC()
                if S.R and mouse.Hit then
                    S.R.CFrame = mouse.Hit + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                end
            end
        end)
    end)
end

function C.ClickTP(on)
    _G.NW_TPT = on
    if on then C.TPTool() end
    NT("ClickTP", on and "ON (Click to TP)" or "OFF", 2)
end

function C.Gravity(val)
    WS.Gravity = val
    NT("Gravity", tostring(val), 2)
end

function C.ResetGravity()
    WS.Gravity = 196.2
    NT("Gravity", "Reset", 2)
end

function C.ResetCamera()
    pcall(function()
        -- Disable all aim locks
        _G.NW_Aim = false
        Aim.Enabled = false
        Aim.Locked = false
        Aim.CurrentTarget = nil
        if Aim.FOVCircle then Aim.FOVCircle.Visible = false end
        
        -- Reset camera
        GC()
        Cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
        if S.H then
            Cam.CameraSubject = S.H
        end
    end)
    NT("Camera", "Reset", 2)
end

function C.CleanupSelf()
    pcall(function()
        GC()
        if S.C then
            for _, child in pairs(S.C:GetDescendants()) do
                if child:IsA("Highlight") or child:IsA("PointLight") or child:IsA("SpotLight") or child:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
                    child:Destroy()
                end
                if child:IsA("BodyVelocity") or child:IsA("BodyGyro") or child:IsA("BodyPosition") or child:IsA("BodyAngularVelocity") then
                    child:Destroy()
                end
            end
            -- Reset HRP size if somehow modified
            if S.R then
                S.R.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1)
                S.R.Transparency = 1
                S.R.CanCollide = false
            end
            -- Reset Head transparency
            local head = S.C:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if head then
                head.Transparency = 0
            end
        end
    end)
    C.ResetCamera()
    NT("Cleanup", "Done", 2)
end

function C.BTools()
    pcall(function()
        local bt = Instance.new("HopperBin")
        bt.BinType = Enum.BinType.Hammer
        bt.Parent = P.Backpack
        
        local bt2 = Instance.new("HopperBin")
        bt2.BinType = Enum.BinType.Clone
        bt2.Parent = P.Backpack
        
        local bt3 = Instance.new("HopperBin")
        bt3.BinType = Enum.BinType.Grab
        bt3.Parent = P.Backpack
    end)
    NT("BTools", "Given", 2)
end

function C.InvisChar(on)
    _G.NW_Invis = on
    if not on then
        pcall(function()
            GC()
            if S.C then
                for _, p in pairs(S.C:GetDescendants()) do
                    if p:IsA("BasePart") then
                        p.Transparency = 0
                    end
                end
                local face = S.C:FindFirstChild("Head") and S.C.Head:FindFirstChild("face")
                if face then face.Transparency = 0 end
            end
        end)
        NT("Invisible", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    pcall(function()
        GC()
        if S.C then
            for _, p in pairs(S.C:GetDescendants()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
                    p.Transparency = 1
                end
            end
            local face = S.C:FindFirstChild("Head") and S.C.Head:FindFirstChild("face")
            if face then face.Transparency = 1 end
        end
    end)
    NT("Invisible", "ON", 2)
end

function C.Fling(tgt)
    if not tgt then
        NT("Fling", "No target", 2)
        return
    end
    if not tgt.Character or not tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        NT("Fling", "Target not found", 2)
        return
    end
    
    GC()
    if not S.R or not S.H then
        NT("Fling", "Character error", 2)
        return
    end
    
    _G.NW_Fling = true
    NT("Fling", "Flinging " .. tgt.Name, 2)
    
    local oG = WS.Gravity
    local oW = S.H.WalkSpeed
    local oJ = S.H.JumpPower
    
    WS.Gravity = 0
    S.H.WalkSpeed = 0
    S.H.JumpPower = 0
    
    local s1 = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
    s1.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
    s1.P = 9e9
    s1.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 9e9, 0)
    s1.Parent = S.R
    
    local s2 = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
    s2.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
    s2.P = 9e9
    s2.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -9e9, 0)
    s2.Parent = S.R
    
    for _, p in pairs(S.C:GetDescendants()) do
        if p:IsA("BasePart") then
            p.CanCollide = false
            p.Massless = true
        end
    end
    
    local st = tick()
    while _G.NW_Fling and tick() - st < 3 and S.Run do
        pcall(function()
            if tgt.Character and tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                S.R.CFrame = tgt.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                S.R.Velocity = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
            else
                _G.NW_Fling = false
            end
        end)
        RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
    
    pcall(function()
        s1:Destroy()
        s2:Destroy()
        WS.Gravity = oG
        GC()
        if S.H then
            S.H.WalkSpeed = oW
            S.H.JumpPower = oJ
        end
    end)
    
    _G.NW_Fling = false
    NT("Fling", "Done", 2)
end

function C.StopFling()
    _G.NW_Fling = false
end

function C.FB()
    pcall(function()
        Light.Brightness = 2
        Light.ClockTime = 14
        Light.FogEnd = 1e9
        Light.GlobalShadows = false
        for _, e in pairs(Light:GetDescendants()) do
            if e:IsA("BlurEffect") or e:IsA("ColorCorrectionEffect") or e:IsA("Atmosphere") or e:IsA("DepthOfFieldEffect") then
                e.Enabled = false
            end
        end
    end)
    NT("Fullbright", "ON", 2)
end

function C.AFK(on)
    _G.NW_AFK = on
    if on then
        local vu = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
        P.Idled:Connect(function()
            if _G.NW_AFK then
                vu:CaptureController()
                vu:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
            end
        end)
    end
    NT("AntiAFK", on and "ON" or "OFF", 2)
end

function C.SrvHop()
    NT("Server", "Finding...", 2)
    pcall(function()
        local srv = Http:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. PId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"))
        for _, s in pairs(srv.data or {}) do
            if s.id ~= game.JobId and s.playing < s.maxPlayers then
                TPS:TeleportToPlaceInstance(PId, s.id, P)
                return
            end
        end
    end)
end

function C.Rejoin()
    TPS:Teleport(PId, P)
end

function C.TP(cf)
    GC()
    if S.R and cf then
        S.R.CFrame = cf
    end
end

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- FE BYPASS & REMOTE UTILITIES
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local FE = {}

-- Get all remotes in game
function FE.GetRemotes()
    local remotes = {Events = {}, Functions = {}}
    for _, v in pairs(Rep:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            table.insert(remotes.Events, v)
        elseif v:IsA("RemoteFunction") then
            table.insert(remotes.Functions, v)
        end
    end
    NT("Remotes", #remotes.Events .. " Events, " .. #remotes.Functions .. " Functions", 3)
    return remotes
end

-- Fire all remotes (chaos mode)
function FE.FireAllRemotes()
    pcall(function()
        for _, v in pairs(Rep:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                pcall(function() v:FireServer() end)
            end
        end
    end)
    NT("Fired", "All remotes", 2)
end

-- Remote spy (prints remote calls) - ENHANCED VERSION
_G.NW_RemoteLog = {}
_G.NW_RSpy = false
_G.NW_HttpSpy = false

function FE.RemoteSpy(on)
    _G.NW_RSpy = on
    if not on then NT("RemoteSpy", "OFF", 2) return end
    
    if _G.NW_RSpy_Hooked then
        NT("RemoteSpy", "ON - Check console (F9)", 2)
        return
    end
    _G.NW_RSpy_Hooked = true
    
    -- Helper to format arguments nicely
    local function formatArg(v, depth)
        depth = depth or 0
        if depth > 3 then return "..." end
        
        local t = typeof(v)
        if t == "string" then
            return '"' .. v:sub(1, 100) .. '"'
        elseif t == "Instance" then
            return v:GetFullName()
        elseif t == "table" then
            local parts = {}
            local count = 0
            for k, val in pairs(v) do
                count = count + 1
                if count > 5 then
                    table.insert(parts, "...")
                    break
                end
                table.insert(parts, tostring(k) .. "=" .. formatArg(val, depth + 1))
            end
            return "{" .. table.concat(parts, ", ") .. "}"
        elseif t == "CFrame" then
            return string.format("CFrame(%d,%d,%d)", math.floor(v.X), math.floor(v.Y), math.floor(v.Z))
        elseif t == "Vector3" then
            return string.format("Vector3(%d,%d,%d)", math.floor(v.X), math.floor(v.Y), math.floor(v.Z))
        else
            return tostring(v)
        end
    end
    
    local oldNamecall
    oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
        local method = getnamecallmethod()
        if _G.NW_RSpy then
            if method == "FireServer" or method == "InvokeServer" then
                local args = {...}
                local remotePath = self:GetFullName()
                local remoteName = self.Name
                
                -- Format output
                local output = "\n[REMOTE SPY] ════════════════════════════════"
                output = output .. "\nRemote: " .. remoteName
                output = output .. "\nPath: " .. remotePath
                output = output .. "\nMethod: " .. method
                output = output .. "\nArgs (" .. #args .. "):"
                
                for i, v in pairs(args) do
                    output = output .. "\n  [" .. i .. "] " .. typeof(v) .. " = " .. formatArg(v)
                end
                output = output .. "\n═══════════════════════════════════════════"
                
                print(output)
                
                -- Store in log
                table.insert(_G.NW_RemoteLog, {
                    time = os.time(),
                    remote = remoteName,
                    path = remotePath,
                    method = method,
                    args = args
                })
                
                -- Keep log at reasonable size
                if #_G.NW_RemoteLog > 100 then
                    table.remove(_G.NW_RemoteLog, 1)
                end
            end
        end
        return oldNamecall(self, ...)
    end)
    NT("RemoteSpy", "ON - Check console (F9)", 2)
end

-- HTTP Spy - Logs all HTTP requests
function FE.HttpSpy(on)
    _G.NW_HttpSpy = on
    if not on then NT("HTTP Spy", "OFF", 2) return end
    
    if _G.NW_HttpSpy_Hooked then
        NT("HTTP Spy", "ON - Check console (F9)", 2)
        return
    end
    _G.NW_HttpSpy_Hooked = true
    
    -- Hook request functions if they exist
    if request then
        local oldRequest = request
        request = function(opts)
            if _G.NW_HttpSpy then
                print("\n[HTTP SPY] ════════════════════════════════")
                print("URL: " .. tostring(opts.Url or opts.url or "?"))
                print("Method: " .. tostring(opts.Method or opts.method or "GET"))
                if opts.Body or opts.body then
                    print("Body: " .. tostring(opts.Body or opts.body):sub(1, 200))
                end
                if opts.Headers or opts.headers then
                    print("Headers: " .. tostring(opts.Headers or opts.headers))
                end
                print("═══════════════════════════════════════════")
            end
            return oldRequest(opts)
        end
    end
    
    if http_request then
        local oldHttp = http_request
        http_request = function(opts)
            if _G.NW_HttpSpy then
                print("\n[HTTP SPY] ════════════════════════════════")
                print("URL: " .. tostring(opts.Url or opts.url or "?"))
                print("Method: " .. tostring(opts.Method or opts.method or "GET"))
                print("═══════════════════════════════════════════")
            end
            return oldHttp(opts)
        end
    end
    
    NT("HTTP Spy", "ON - Check console (F9)", 2)
end

-- Export remote log to string (for copying)
function FE.ExportRemoteLog()
    if #_G.NW_RemoteLog == 0 then
        NT("Export", "No remotes logged yet", 2)
        return ""
    end
    
    local output = "-- NexusWare Remote Log Export --\n"
    output = output .. "-- " .. #_G.NW_RemoteLog .. " remotes captured --\n\n"
    
    for _, log in ipairs(_G.NW_RemoteLog) do
        output = output .. "-- Remote: " .. log.remote .. "\n"
        output = output .. "-- Path: " .. log.path .. "\n"
        output = output .. "game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')"
        
        -- Build path
        local pathParts = {}
        for part in log.path:gmatch("[^%.]+") do
            table.insert(pathParts, part)
        end
        for i = 2, #pathParts do
            output = output .. '["' .. pathParts[i] .. '"]'
        end
        
        output = output .. ":" .. log.method .. "("
        -- Args placeholder
        output = output .. "-- args here"
        output = output .. ")\n\n"
    end
    
    if setclipboard then
        setclipboard(output)
        NT("Export", "Copied to clipboard!", 2)
    else
        print(output)
        NT("Export", "Printed to console (no clipboard)", 2)
    end
    
    return output
end

-- Clear remote log
function FE.ClearRemoteLog()
    _G.NW_RemoteLog = {}
    NT("Cleared", "Remote log cleared", 2)
end

-- Simulate player actions via remotes
function FE.SimulateClick()
    pcall(function()
        VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
        task.wait(0.05)
        VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
    end)
end

function FE.SimulateKey(keyCode)
    pcall(function()
        VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, keyCode, false, game)
        task.wait(0.1)
        VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, keyCode, false, game)
    end)
end

-- Network owner exploit (requires specific exploits)
function FE.ClaimNetworkOwnership()
    pcall(function()
        for _, v in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("BasePart") and v:GetNetworkOwner() ~= P then
                pcall(function() v:SetNetworkOwner(P) end)
            end
        end
    end)
    NT("Network", "Claimed ownership", 2)
end

-- Delete parts (client-side)
function FE.DeletePart(partName)
    local count = 0
    for _, v in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name:lower():find(partName:lower()) then
            pcall(function() v:Destroy() count = count + 1 end)
        end
    end
    NT("Deleted", count .. " parts", 2)
end

-- Bring all items to player
function FE.BringItems()
    GC()
    if not S.R then return end
    local count = 0
    for _, v in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
        if v:IsA("Tool") or v:IsA("Model") and v:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
            pcall(function()
                local handle = v:FindFirstChild("Handle") or v:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
                if handle then
                    handle.CFrame = S.R.CFrame
                    count = count + 1
                end
            end)
        end
    end
    NT("Brought", count .. " items", 2)
end

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- ADVANCED UNIVERSAL FUNCTIONS
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local Adv = {}

-- Teleport to player
function Adv.TPToPlayer(playerName)
    for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if p.Name:lower():find(playerName:lower()) and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            C.TP(p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
            NT("TP", "To " .. p.Name, 2)
            return
        end
    end
    NT("TP", "Player not found", 2)
end

-- Bring player to you (client-side visual only)
function Adv.BringPlayer(playerName)
    GC()
    if not S.R then return end
    for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if p.Name:lower():find(playerName:lower()) and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            pcall(function()
                p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = S.R.CFrame + Vector3.new(3, 0, 0)
            end)
            NT("Bring", p.Name, 2)
            return
        end
    end
    NT("Bring", "Player not found", 2)
end

-- Orbit player
function Adv.Orbit(playerName, radius)
    _G.NW_Orbit = true
    _G.NW_OrbitRadius = radius or 10
    local target
    for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if p.Name:lower():find(playerName:lower()) then target = p break end
    end
    if not target then NT("Orbit", "Player not found", 2) return end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        local angle = 0
        while _G.NW_Orbit and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and S.R then
                    angle = angle + 0.1
                    local x = math.cos(angle) * _G.NW_OrbitRadius
                    local z = math.sin(angle) * _G.NW_OrbitRadius
                    S.R.CFrame = target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(x, 0, z)
                end
            end)
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("Orbit", target.Name, 2)
end

function Adv.StopOrbit()
    _G.NW_Orbit = false
    NT("Orbit", "Stopped", 2)
end

-- Attach to player
function Adv.Attach(playerName)
    _G.NW_Attach = true
    local target
    for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if p.Name:lower():find(playerName:lower()) then target = p break end
    end
    if not target then NT("Attach", "Player not found", 2) return end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_Attach and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and S.R then
                    S.R.CFrame = target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                end
            end)
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("Attach", target.Name, 2)
end

function Adv.Detach()
    _G.NW_Attach = false
    NT("Attach", "Detached", 2)
end

-- Freeze character
function Adv.Freeze(on)
    _G.NW_Freeze = on
    if not on then
        pcall(function()
            GC()
            if S.R then
                local bp = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_FreezePos")
                if bp then bp:Destroy() end
            end
        end)
        NT("Freeze", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    GC()
    if S.R then
        local bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
        bp.Name = "NW_FreezePos"
        bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bp.Position = S.R.Position
        bp.Parent = S.R
    end
    NT("Freeze", "ON", 2)
end

-- Spin character
function Adv.Spin(on, speed)
    _G.NW_Spin = on
    _G.NW_SpinSpeed = speed or 50
    if not on then
        pcall(function()
            GC()
            if S.R then
                local av = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_SpinAV")
                if av then av:Destroy() end
            end
        end)
        NT("Spin", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    GC()
    if S.R then
        local av = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
        av.Name = "NW_SpinAV"
        av.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
        av.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, _G.NW_SpinSpeed, 0)
        av.Parent = S.R
    end
    NT("Spin", "ON", 2)
end

-- Platform (spawn a platform under player)
function Adv.Platform()
    GC()
    if not S.R then return end
    local p = Instance.new("Part")
    p.Name = "NW_Platform"
    p.Size = Vector3.new(20, 1, 20)
    p.Position = S.R.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
    p.Anchored = true
    p.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Bright green")
    p.Parent = WS
    NT("Platform", "Created", 2)
end

-- Remove platforms
function Adv.RemovePlatforms()
    for _, v in pairs(WS:GetChildren()) do
        if v.Name == "NW_Platform" then v:Destroy() end
    end
    NT("Platform", "Removed", 2)
end

-- Annoy player (follow and jump around them)
function Adv.Annoy(playerName)
    _G.NW_Annoy = true
    local target
    for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if p.Name:lower():find(playerName:lower()) then target = p break end
    end
    if not target then NT("Annoy", "Player not found", 2) return end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_Annoy and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and S.R then
                    local offset = Vector3.new(math.random(-5, 5), 0, math.random(-5, 5))
                    S.R.CFrame = target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame + offset
                    if S.H then S.H.Jump = true end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.3)
        end
    end)
    NT("Annoy", target.Name, 2)
end

function Adv.StopAnnoy()
    _G.NW_Annoy = false
    NT("Annoy", "Stopped", 2)
end

-- Copy player appearance
function Adv.CopyPlayer(playerName)
    for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if p.Name:lower():find(playerName:lower()) and p.Character then
            pcall(function()
                local desc = Plrs:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(p.UserId)
                GC()
                if S.H then S.H:ApplyDescription(desc) end
            end)
            NT("Copy", p.Name .. "'s appearance", 2)
            return
        end
    end
    NT("Copy", "Player not found", 2)
end

-- Reset appearance
function Adv.ResetAppearance()
    pcall(function()
        local desc = Plrs:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(P.UserId)
        GC()
        if S.H then S.H:ApplyDescription(desc) end
    end)
    NT("Reset", "Appearance", 2)
end

            print("[PlayerInfo] " .. info)
            NT("PlayerInfo", p.Name .. " - Age: " .. p.AccountAge .. "d", 4)
            return
        end
    end
    NT("PlayerInfo", "Player not found", 2)
end

-- Chat spam
function Adv.ChatSpam(message, interval)
    _G.NW_ChatSpam = true
    _G.NW_ChatMsg = message or "NexusWare Pro"
    _G.NW_ChatInt = interval or 2
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_ChatSpam and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                local chatRemote = Rep:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
                if chatRemote then
                    local say = chatRemote:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")
                    if say then say:FireServer(_G.NW_ChatMsg, "All") end
                else
                    game:GetService("Chat"):Chat(P.Character, _G.NW_ChatMsg, Enum.ChatColor.White)
                end
            end)
            SW(_G.NW_ChatInt)
        end
    end)
    NT("ChatSpam", "ON", 2)
end

function Adv.StopChatSpam()
    _G.NW_ChatSpam = false
    NT("ChatSpam", "OFF", 2)
end

-- View player (spectate)
function Adv.View(playerName)
    for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if p.Name:lower():find(playerName:lower()) and p.Character then
            WS.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            NT("View", p.Name, 2)
            return
        end
    end
    NT("View", "Player not found", 2)
end

function Adv.Unview()
    GC()
    if S.H then
        WS.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = S.H
    end
    NT("View", "Reset", 2)
end

-- Kill character (client-side)
function Adv.Kill()
    GC()
    if S.H then S.H.Health = 0 end
end

-- Respawn
function Adv.Respawn()
    P:LoadCharacter()
end

-- Goto random player
function Adv.GotoRandom()
    local players = Plrs:GetPlayers()
    local others = {}
    for _, p in pairs(players) do
        if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            table.insert(others, p)
        end
    end
    if #others > 0 then
        local target = others[math.random(1, #others)]
        C.TP(target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
        NT("TP", "To " .. target.Name, 2)
    else
        NT("TP", "No players found", 2)
    end
end

-- Rejoin with saved position
_G.NW_SavedPos = nil
function Adv.SavePosition()
    GC()
    if S.R then
        _G.NW_SavedPos = S.R.CFrame
        NT("Position", "Saved", 2)
    end
end

function Adv.LoadPosition()
    if _G.NW_SavedPos then
        C.TP(_G.NW_SavedPos)
        NT("Position", "Loaded", 2)
    else
        NT("Position", "No saved position", 2)
    end
end

-- X-Ray (make all parts transparent)
function Adv.XRay(on)
    _G.NW_XRay = on
    if on then
        for _, v in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Transparency < 0.5 then
                v:SetAttribute("NW_OrigTrans", v.Transparency)
                v.Transparency = 0.7
            end
        end
        NT("X-Ray", "ON", 2)
    else
        for _, v in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("BasePart") and v:GetAttribute("NW_OrigTrans") then
                v.Transparency = v:GetAttribute("NW_OrigTrans")
                v:SetAttribute("NW_OrigTrans", nil)
            end
        end
        NT("X-Ray", "OFF", 2)
    end
end

-- No fog
function Adv.NoFog()
    pcall(function()
        Light.FogStart = 0
        Light.FogEnd = 1e9
        for _, v in pairs(Light:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("Atmosphere") then v.Density = 0 end
        end
    end)
    NT("NoFog", "Applied", 2)
end

-- Day time
function Adv.Day()
    pcall(function() Light.ClockTime = 14 end)
    NT("Time", "Day", 2)
end

-- Night time
function Adv.Night()
    pcall(function() Light.ClockTime = 0 end)
    NT("Time", "Night", 2)
end

-- Remove skybox
function Adv.RemoveSkybox()
    pcall(function()
        for _, v in pairs(Light:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("Sky") then v:Destroy() end
        end
    end)
    NT("Skybox", "Removed", 2)
end

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- MM2 MODULE (Enhanced)
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local MM2 = {
    Roles = {},
    Settings = {
        AutoGrabDelay = 0.5
    }
}

function MM2.UpdateRoles()
    MM2.Roles = {}
    for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        pcall(function()
            local hasKnife = (p.Backpack and p.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Knife")) or (p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("Knife"))
            local hasGun = (p.Backpack and p.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Gun")) or (p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("Gun"))
            local hasHero = (p.Backpack and p.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Hero")) or (p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("Hero"))
            
            if hasKnife then
                MM2.Roles.Murderer = p
            elseif hasGun then
                MM2.Roles.Sheriff = p
            elseif hasHero then
                MM2.Roles.Hero = p
            end
        end)
    end
end

function MM2.RoleESP(on)
    _G.NW_RE = on
    if not on then
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
            pcall(function()
                if p.Character then
                    local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_RE")
                    if h then h:Destroy() end
                end
            end)
        end
        NT("RoleESP", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_RE and S.Run do
            MM2.UpdateRoles()
            for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                pcall(function()
                    if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        local old = p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_RE")
                        if old then old:Destroy() end
                        
                        local color = Color3.new(0, 1, 0) -- Innocent
                        if p == MM2.Roles.Murderer then
                            color = Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
                        elseif p == MM2.Roles.Sheriff then
                            color = Color3.new(0, 0.4, 1)
                        elseif p == MM2.Roles.Hero then
                            color = Color3.new(1, 1, 0)
                        end
                        CreateHighlight(p.Character, "NW_RE", color)
                    end
                end)
            end
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("RoleESP", "ON", 2)
end

function MM2.CoinESP(on)
    _G.NW_CE = on
    if not on then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            pcall(function()
                if o.Name == "NW_CE" then o:Destroy() end
            end)
        end
        NT("CoinESP", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_CE and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if o:IsA("BasePart") and o.Name:find("Coin") then
                        if not o:FindFirstChild("NW_CE") then
                            CreateHighlight(o, "NW_CE", Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("CoinESP", "ON", 2)
end

    _G.NW_TE = on
    if not on then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            pcall(function()
                if o.Name == "NW_TE" then o:Destroy() end
            end)
        end

    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_TE and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if o.Name:lower():find("trap") then
                        if not o:FindFirstChild("NW_TE") then
                            CreateHighlight(o, "NW_TE", Color3.new(1, 0.5, 0))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("TrapESP", "ON", 2)
end

    _G.NW_GE = on
    if not on then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            pcall(function()
                if o.Name == "NW_GE" then o:Destroy() end

    task.spawn(function()

function MM2.AutoCoins(on)
    _G.NW_AC = on
    if not on then
        NT("AutoCoins", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_AC and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.R and firetouchinterest then
                    for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                        if o:IsA("BasePart") and o.Name:find("Coin") then
                            firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 0)
                            firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 1)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.3)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoCoins", "ON", 2)
end

function MM2.GrabGun()
    pcall(function()
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if o.Name == "GunDrop" then
                GC()
                if S.R then
                    local origPos = S.R.CFrame
                    S.R.CFrame = o.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    if firetouchinterest then
                        firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 0)
                        firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 1)
                    end
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    S.R.CFrame = origPos
                    NT("GrabGun", "Done", 2)
                    return
                end
            end
        end
        NT("GrabGun", "No gun found", 2)
    end)
end

function MM2.AutoGrabGun(on)
    _G.NW_AGG = on
    if not on then NT("AutoGrabGun", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_AGG and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                MM2.UpdateRoles()
                if not MM2.Roles.Sheriff and not MM2.Roles.Hero then
                    for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                        if o.Name == "GunDrop" then
                            GC()
                            if S.R then
                                local origPos = S.R.CFrame
                                S.R.CFrame = o.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
                                task.wait(0.05)
                                if firetouchinterest then
                                    firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 0)
                                    firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 1)
                                end
                                task.wait(0.05)
                                S.R.CFrame = origPos
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(MM2.Settings.AutoGrabDelay)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoGrabGun", "ON", 2)
end

function MM2.XRay(on)
    _G.NW_XR = on
    if not on then
        pcall(function()
            for _, p in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                if p:IsA("BasePart") and p.Transparency > 0 and p.Transparency < 1 then
                    if p:GetAttribute("NW_OrigTrans") then
                        p.Transparency = p:GetAttribute("NW_OrigTrans")
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        NT("XRay", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_XR and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, p in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if p:IsA("BasePart") and not p:IsDescendantOf(P.Character) then
                        local plr = Plrs:GetPlayerFromCharacter(p:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model"))
                        if not plr then
                            if not p:GetAttribute("NW_OrigTrans") then
                                p:SetAttribute("NW_OrigTrans", p.Transparency)
                            end
                            p.Transparency = 0.7
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("XRay", "ON", 2)
end

function MM2.NotifyRoles(on)
    _G.NW_NR = on
    if not on then NT("NotifyRoles", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        local lastMurderer, lastSheriff = nil, nil
        while _G.NW_NR and S.Run do
            MM2.UpdateRoles()
            if MM2.Roles.Murderer and MM2.Roles.Murderer ~= lastMurderer then
                NT("MURDERER", MM2.Roles.Murderer.Name, 5)
                lastMurderer = MM2.Roles.Murderer
            end
            if MM2.Roles.Sheriff and MM2.Roles.Sheriff ~= lastSheriff then
                NT("SHERIFF", MM2.Roles.Sheriff.Name, 5)
                lastSheriff = MM2.Roles.Sheriff
            end
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("NotifyRoles", "ON", 2)
end

function MM2.TPMurderer()
    MM2.UpdateRoles()
    if MM2.Roles.Murderer and MM2.Roles.Murderer.Character then
        local hrp = MM2.Roles.Murderer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            GC()
            if S.R then
                S.R.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
                NT("TP", "Murderer", 2)
            end
        end
    else
        NT("TP", "No murderer found", 2)
    end
end

    MM2.UpdateRoles()

            if S.R then
                S.R.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
                NT("TP", "Sheriff", 2)
            end
        end
    else
        NT("TP", "No sheriff found", 2)
    end

    if not on then
        NT("GodMode", "OFF", 2)
        return

    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_GM and S.Run do

                end
            end)

    NT("GodMode", "ON", 2)

function MM2.BringPlayers(on)
    _G.NW_BP = on
    if not on then NT("BringPlayers", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BP and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.R then
                    for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                        if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                            p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = S.R.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 5)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("BringPlayers", "ON", 2)
end

        NT("TP", "Lobby", 2)
    end

function MM2.TPMap()
    GC()
    if S.R then
        pcall(function()
            local map = WS:FindFirstChild("Map")
            if map then
                local spawn = map:FindFirstChild("SpawnLocation") or map:FindFirstChildOfClass("SpawnLocation")
                if spawn then
                    S.R.CFrame = spawn.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
                    NT("TP", "Map Spawn", 2)
                    return
                end
            end
        end)
        NT("TP", "Map not found", 2)
    end
end

    NT("JumpPower", tostring(val), 2)
