function MM2.Fly(on)
    _G.NW_MM2Fly = on
    if not on then
        pcall(function()
            GC()
            if S.R then
                local bv = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_MM2FlyBV")
                local bg = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_MM2FlyBG")
                if bv then bv:Destroy() end
                if bg then bg:Destroy() end
            end
        end)
        NT("Fly", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        GC()
        if not S.R then return end
        local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        bv.Name = "NW_MM2FlyBV"
        bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bv.Parent = S.R
        local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
        bg.Name = "NW_MM2FlyBG"
        bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bg.P = 10000
        bg.Parent = S.R
        while _G.NW_MM2Fly and S.Run do
            bv.Velocity = Cam.CFrame.LookVector * (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) and 80 or 0)
                + Cam.CFrame.RightVector * ((UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) and 80 or 0) - (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) and 80 or 0))
                + Vector3.new(0, (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) and 80 or 0) - (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) and 80 or 0), 0)
            bg.CFrame = Cam.CFrame
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("Fly", "ON", 2)
end

    _G.NW_MM2NC = on
    if not on then NT("Noclip", "OFF", 2) return end

        end)
    end)
    NT("InfJump", "ON", 2)
end

function MM2.Aimbot(on)
    _G.NW_MM2Aim = on
    if not on then NT("Aimbot", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_MM2Aim and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                MM2.UpdateRoles()
                local target = MM2.Roles.Murderer
                if target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
                    Cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Cam.CFrame.Position, target.Character.Head.Position)
                end
            end)
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("Aimbot", "ON (Targets Murderer)", 2)
end

function MM2.AutoShoot(on)
    _G.NW_MM2Shoot = on
    if not on then NT("AutoShoot", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_MM2Shoot and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                MM2.UpdateRoles()
                if MM2.Roles.Murderer and MM2.Roles.Murderer.Character then
                    GC()
                    if S.R then
                        local dist = Dst(S.R.Position, MM2.Roles.Murderer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
                        if dist < 50 then
                            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                            task.wait(0.05)
                            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.2)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoShoot", "ON", 2)
end

function MM2.AntiMurderer(on)
    _G.NW_MM2Anti = on
    if not on then NT("AntiMurderer", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_MM2Anti and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                MM2.UpdateRoles()
                if MM2.Roles.Murderer and MM2.Roles.Murderer.Character then
                    GC()
                    if S.R then
                        local dist = Dst(S.R.Position, MM2.Roles.Murderer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
                        if dist < 15 then
                            local awayDir = (S.R.Position - MM2.Roles.Murderer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Unit
                            S.R.CFrame = S.R.CFrame + (awayDir * 20)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("AntiMurderer", "ON", 2)
end

function MM2.Spectate(player)
    if player and player.Character then
        Cam.CameraSubject = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        NT("Spectate", player.Name, 2)
    end
end

function MM2.Unspectate()
    GC()
    if S.H then Cam.CameraSubject = S.H end
    NT("Spectate", "Self", 2)
end

    if MM2.Roles.Murderer then
        MM2.Spectate(MM2.Roles.Murderer)
    else

    end

    if not on then NT("AntiAFK", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_MM2AFK and S.Run do
            pcall(function()

            end)

function MM2.Hitbox(size)
    _G.NW_MM2HB = size or 10
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_MM2HB and _G.NW_MM2HB > 0 and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= P and p.Character then
                        local hrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if hrp then
                            hrp.Size = Vector3.new(_G.NW_MM2HB, _G.NW_MM2HB, _G.NW_MM2HB)
                            hrp.Transparency = 1
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("Hitbox", tostring(size), 2)
end

function MM2.Fullbright()
    pcall(function()
        Light.Ambient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        Light.Brightness = 2
        Light.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        for _, v in pairs(Light:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("Atmosphere") or v:IsA("BloomEffect") or v:IsA("BlurEffect") then
                v.Enabled = false
            end
        end
    end)
    NT("Fullbright", "ON", 2)
end

function Fi.FindRod()
    GC()
    if S.C then
        local tool = S.C:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
        if tool and (tool:FindFirstChild("values") or tool.Name:lower():find("rod")) then
            return tool
        end
    end
    for _, t in pairs(P.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
        if t:IsA("Tool") and (t:FindFirstChild("values") or t.Name:lower():find("rod")) then
            return t
        end
    end
    return nil
end

function Fi.EquipRod()
    GC()
    local rod = Fi.FindRod()
    if rod and rod.Parent == P.Backpack and S.H then
        S.H:EquipTool(rod)
        task.wait(0.3)
        return true
    end
    return rod ~= nil
end

-- ===== ELITE AUTOFISH 2026 (Works with UI open, no mouse interference) =====
function Fi.AutoFish(on)
    _G.NW_AF = on
    if not on then return end
    
    NT("AutoFish", "ON - Works with UI open!", 2)
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_AF and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                -- Step 1: Equip rod if needed
                if not Fi.EquipRod() then
                    task.wait(1)
                    return
                end
                
                local rod = Fi.FindRod()
                if not rod then task.wait(0.5) return end
                
                local pg = P:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                if not pg then task.wait(0.1) return end
                
                -- ===== DETECT GAME STATE =====
                local shakeBtn = nil
                local reelBar = nil
                local playerBar = nil
                local fishBar = nil
                
                for _, gui in pairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
                    if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and gui.Enabled ~= false then
                        -- Find shake button
                        if gui.Name:lower():find("shake") or gui.Name == "shakeui" then
                            for _, d in pairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                                if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and d.Visible then
                                    shakeBtn = d
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        
                        -- Find reel minigame elements
                        for _, d in pairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                            if d:IsA("Frame") and d.Visible then
                                local name = d.Name:lower()
                                if name:find("playerbar") or name:find("player") or name:find("reel") then
                                    if d.AbsoluteSize.X < 100 and d.AbsoluteSize.Y < 100 then
                                        playerBar = d
                                    end
                                end
                                if name:find("fish") or name:find("target") or name:find("catch") then
                                    fishBar = d
                                end
                                if name:find("bar") and d.AbsoluteSize.X > 200 then
                                    reelBar = d
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                
                -- ===== STATE 1: SHAKE MINIGAME =====
                if shakeBtn then
                    -- Click shake button using remote event or direct method
                    pcall(function()
                        -- Try multiple methods to click
                        if shakeBtn.MouseButton1Click then
                            for _, conn in pairs(getconnections and getconnections(shakeBtn.MouseButton1Click) or {}) do
                                pcall(function() conn:Fire() end)
                            end
                        end
                        if shakeBtn.Activated then
                            for _, conn in pairs(getconnections and getconnections(shakeBtn.Activated) or {}) do
                                pcall(function() conn:Fire() end)
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                    -- Fallback: use keypress simulation (E key usually works)
                    pcall(function()
                        local ContextAction = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
                        -- Trigger any bound action
                        VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                        task.wait(0.01)
                        VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                    end)
                    task.wait(0.02)
                    return
                end
                
                -- ===== STATE 2: REEL MINIGAME (Move bar to fish) =====
                if playerBar and reelBar then
                    -- The reel minigame: hold click to move playerBar up to where fishBar is
                    -- We need to fire the rod's reel event instead of using mouse
                    
                    -- Method 1: Use rod events directly
                    if rod:FindFirstChild("events") then
                        local reelEvent = rod.events:FindFirstChild("reel") or rod.events:FindFirstChild("reeling")
                        if reelEvent and reelEvent:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                            reelEvent:FireServer(true) -- true = reeling
                        end
                    end
                    
                    -- Method 2: Use ReplicatedStorage events
                    local repEvents = Rep:FindFirstChild("events") or Rep:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                    if repEvents then
                        for _, e in pairs(repEvents:GetChildren()) do
                            if e:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                                local name = e.Name:lower()
                                if name:find("reel") then
                                    pcall(function() e:FireServer(true) end)
                                    pcall(function() e:FireServer(1) end)
                                    pcall(function() e:FireServer() end)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    
                    -- Method 3: Simulate spacebar (some games use this)
                    VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
                    
                    task.wait(0.03)
                    return
                end
                
                -- ===== STATE 3: NEED TO CAST =====
                local vals = rod:FindFirstChild("values")
                local needCast = true
                if vals then
                    local lure = vals:FindFirstChild("lure") or vals:FindFirstChild("castvalue") or vals:FindFirstChild("bobber")
                    if lure and lure:IsA("NumberValue") and lure.Value > 0.5 then
                        needCast = false
                    end
                end
                
                if needCast then
                    -- Cast using rod events (doesn't interfere with UI)
                    if rod:FindFirstChild("events") then
                        local castEvent = rod.events:FindFirstChild("cast") or rod.events:FindFirstChild("throw")
                        if castEvent and castEvent:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                            castEvent:FireServer(100, 1) -- max power, direction
                            task.wait(0.5)
                            return
                        end
                    end
                    
                    -- Fallback: use UserInputService simulation
                    local castPower = 100
                    pcall(function()
                        -- Simulate holding and releasing mouse without blocking
                        game:GetService("UserInputService").InputBegan:Fire({
                            UserInputType = Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1,
                            UserInputState = Enum.UserInputState.Begin
                        })
                    end)
                    task.wait(0.7)
                    pcall(function()
                        game:GetService("UserInputService").InputEnded:Fire({
                            UserInputType = Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1,
                            UserInputState = Enum.UserInputState.End
                        })
                    end)
                    task.wait(0.3)
                end
            end)
            task.wait(0.05)
        end
    end)
end

-- ===== AUTOSHAKE (Works without mouse) =====
function Fi.AutoShake(on)
    _G.NW_AS = on
    if not on then return end
    
    NT("AutoShake", "ON", 2)
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_AS and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                local pg = P:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                if pg then
                    for _, gui in pairs(pg:GetChildren()) do
                        if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and gui.Enabled ~= false then
                            if gui.Name:lower():find("shake") then
                                for _, d in pairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
                                    if (d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("ImageButton")) and d.Visible then
                                        -- Use keyboard instead of mouse
                                        VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                                        task.wait(0.01)
                                        VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            task.wait(0.02)
        end
    end)
end

-- ===== AUTOREEL (Handles the bar minigame) =====
function Fi.AutoReel(on)
    _G.NW_AR = on
    if not on then return end
    
    NT("AutoReel", "ON", 2)
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_AR and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                local rod = Fi.FindRod()
                if not rod then return end
                
                -- Method 1: Fire rod reel event
                if rod:FindFirstChild("events") then
                    local reelEvent = rod.events:FindFirstChild("reel") or rod.events:FindFirstChild("reeling")
                    if reelEvent and reelEvent:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                        reelEvent:FireServer(true)
                        reelEvent:FireServer(1)
                    end
                end
                
                -- Method 2: Fire ReplicatedStorage reel events
                local repEvents = Rep:FindFirstChild("events") or Rep:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                if repEvents then
                    for _, e in pairs(repEvents:GetChildren()) do
                        if e:IsA("RemoteEvent") and e.Name:lower():find("reel") then
                            pcall(function() e:FireServer(true) end)
                            pcall(function() e:FireServer(1) end)
                        end
                    end
                end
                
                -- Method 3: Keyboard input for reel
                VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
                task.wait(0.03)
                VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
            end)
            task.wait(0.03)
        end
    end)
end

-- ===== AUTOSELL =====
function Fi.AutoSell(on)
    _G.NW_ASe = on
    if not on then return end
    
    NT("AutoSell", "ON", 2)
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_ASe and S.Run do

                local events = Rep:FindFirstChild("events")
                if events then
                    for _, e in pairs(events:GetChildren()) do
                        if e:IsA("RemoteEvent") and e.Name:lower():find("sell") then
                            e:FireServer("all")
                            e:FireServer()
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            task.wait(3)
        end
    end)
end

-- ===== AUTOCAST (Cast only, no mouse) =====
function Fi.AutoCast(on)
    _G.NW_AC = on
    if not on then return end
    
    NT("AutoCast", "ON", 2)
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_AC and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                local rod = Fi.FindRod()
                if rod and rod:FindFirstChild("values") then
                    local lure = rod.values:FindFirstChild("lure")
                    if lure and lure.Value <= 0.01 then
                        if rod:FindFirstChild("events") and rod.events:FindFirstChild("cast") then
                            rod.events.cast:FireServer(100, 1)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end

-- ===== AUTOREEL =====
function Fi.AutoReel(on)
    _G.NW_AR = on
    if not on then return end
    
    task.spawn(function()

            pcall(function()
                local rod = Fi.FindRod()

                    if lure and lure.Value >= 90 then
                        local events = Rep:FindFirstChild("events")
                        if events and events:FindFirstChild("reelfinished") then
                            events.reelfinished:FireServer(100, true)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
    end)

-- ===== IMPROVED INFINITE OXYGEN =====
function Fi.InfOxygen(on)
    _G.NW_IO = on
    if not on then return end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_IO and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                -- Method 1: PlayerGui values
                local pg = P:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                if pg then
                    for _, v in pairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do
                        if v:IsA("NumberValue") or v:IsA("IntValue") then
                            if v.Name:lower():find("oxygen") or v.Name:lower():find("air") or v.Name:lower():find("breath") then
                                v.Value = 100
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                -- Method 2: Player data
                local pdata = P:FindFirstChild("PlayerData") or P:FindFirstChild("Data") or P:FindFirstChild("Stats")
                if pdata then
                    for _, v in pairs(pdata:GetDescendants()) do
                        if v.Name:lower():find("oxygen") and (v:IsA("NumberValue") or v:IsA("IntValue")) then
                            v.Value = 100
                        end
                    end
                end
                -- Method 3: Character values
                GC()
                if S.C then
                    for _, v in pairs(S.C:GetDescendants()) do
                        if v.Name:lower():find("oxygen") and (v:IsA("NumberValue") or v:IsA("IntValue")) then
                            v.Value = 100
                        end
                    end
                end
                -- Method 4: ReplicatedStorage
                for _, v in pairs(Rep:GetDescendants()) do
                    if v.Name == P.Name or (v.Parent and v.Parent.Name == P.Name) then
                        if v.Name:lower():find("oxygen") and (v:IsA("NumberValue") or v:IsA("IntValue")) then
                            pcall(function() v.Value = 100 end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
    NT("InfOxygen", "ON", 2)
end

-- ===== IMPROVED WALK ON WATER =====
function Fi.WalkWater(on)
    _G.NW_WW = on
    if not on then return end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        local waterLevel = 131
        while _G.NW_WW and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.R then
                    local pos = S.R.Position
                    -- Only apply if below water level and not too deep
                    if pos.Y < waterLevel and pos.Y > waterLevel - 50 then
                        S.R.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos.X, waterLevel, pos.Z) * CFrame.Angles(S.R.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesXYZ())
                        if S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_WW_BP") then
                            S.R.NW_WW_BP:Destroy()
                        end
                        local bp = Instance.new("BodyPosition")
                        bp.Name = "NW_WW_BP"
                        bp.MaxForce = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
                        bp.Position = Vector3.new(pos.X, waterLevel, pos.Z)
                        bp.Parent = S.R
                        task.delay(0.1, function()
                            if bp and bp.Parent then bp:Destroy() end
                        end)
                    end
                end
            end)
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
        -- Cleanup
        pcall(function()
            if S.R and S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_WW_BP") then
                S.R.NW_WW_BP:Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end)
    NT("WalkWater", "ON", 2)
end

-- ===== IMPROVED BOAT SPEED =====
function Fi.BoatSpeed(mult)
    _G.NW_BSMult = mult or 2
    pcall(function()
        -- Find player's boat
        for _, boat in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if boat:IsA("Model") and boat:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") then
                local owner = boat:FindFirstChild("Owner") or boat:FindFirstChild("owner")
                local isOwned = false
                if owner then
                    if owner:IsA("StringValue") and owner.Value == P.Name then isOwned = true end
                    if owner:IsA("ObjectValue") and owner.Value == P then isOwned = true end
                end
                -- Also check if player is sitting in it
                local seat = boat:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat")
                if seat and seat.Occupant then
                    local h = seat.Occupant
                    if h.Parent == S.C then isOwned = true end
                end
                
                if isOwned then
                    for _, part in pairs(boat:GetDescendants()) do
                        if part:IsA("VehicleSeat") then
                            part.MaxSpeed = 50 * mult
                            part.Torque = 50000 * mult
                        end
                        if part:IsA("BodyVelocity") or part:IsA("BodyForce") then
                            part.MaxForce = part.MaxForce * mult
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    NT("BoatSpeed", mult .. "x Applied", 2)
end

-- ===== IMPROVED ANTI AFK =====
function Fi.AntiAFK(on)
    _G.NW_AAFK = on
    if not on then return end
    
    -- Disable the AFK kick (if getconnections available)
    pcall(function()
        if getconnections then
            for _, c in pairs(getconnections(P.Idled)) do
                c:Disable()
            end
        end
    end)
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_AAFK and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                -- Send input to prevent AFK
                VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.W, false, game)
                task.wait(0.05)
                VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.W, false, game)
                -- Also fire anti-idle
                local VRService = game:GetService("VirtualUser")
                if VRService then
                    pcall(function() VRService:CaptureController() end)
                    pcall(function() VRService:ClickButton2(Vector2.new()) end)
                end
            end)
            task.wait(30)
        end
    end)
    NT("AntiAFK", "ON", 2)
end

-- ===== AUTO EQUIP BEST ROD =====
function Fi.AutoEquipBestRod(on)
    _G.NW_AEBR = on
    if not on then return end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_AEBR and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.H and not S.C:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
                    local bestRod = nil
                    local bestValue = 0
                    local rarityValues = {["Mythical"] = 6, ["Legendary"] = 5, ["Epic"] = 4, ["Rare"] = 3, ["Uncommon"] = 2, ["Common"] = 1}
                    
                    for _, tool in pairs(P.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
                        if tool:IsA("Tool") and (tool:FindFirstChild("values") or tool.Name:lower():find("rod")) then
                            local val = 0
                            for rarity, v in pairs(rarityValues) do
                                if tool.Name:find(rarity) then
                                    val = v
                                    break
                                end
                            end
                            if val > bestValue then
                                bestValue = val
                                bestRod = tool
                            elseif val == 0 and not bestRod then
                                bestRod = tool
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    
                    if bestRod then
                        S.H:EquipTool(bestRod)
                    end
                end
            end)
            task.wait(2)
        end
    end)
end

-- ===== HELPER: CLICK PROXIMITY PROMPT =====
local function ClickNearbyPrompt()
    task.wait(0.5)
    pcall(function()
        GC()
        if not S.R then return end
        for _, v in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("ProximityPrompt") then
                local dist = Dst(S.R.Position, v.Parent and v.Parent:IsA("BasePart") and v.Parent.Position or Vector3.new(0,0,0))
                if dist < 15 then
                    -- Try fireproximityprompt if available
                    if fireproximityprompt then
                        pcall(function() fireproximityprompt(v, 1) end)
                    else
                        -- Fallback: trigger prompt directly
                        pcall(function() v:InputHoldBegin() task.wait(v.HoldDuration + 0.1) v:InputHoldEnd() end)
                    end
                    return
                end
            end
        end
        -- Also try clicking E key for legacy prompts
        VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
        task.wait(0.1)
        VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
    end)
end

-- BLOX FRUITS MODULE
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local BF = {}
BF.Loc = {
    ["Starter"] = CFrame.new(1049, 15, 1566),
    ["Jungle"] = CFrame.new(-1612, 37, 152),
    ["Pirate"] = CFrame.new(-1183, 5, 3803),
    ["Desert"] = CFrame.new(924, 6, 4405),
    ["Frozen"] = CFrame.new(1137, 16, -1176),
    ["Marine"] = CFrame.new(-4846, 22, 4282),
    ["Skylands"] = CFrame.new(-4869, 717, -2622),
    ["Rose"] = CFrame.new(-391, 6, -3609),
    ["PortTown"] = CFrame.new(-290, 44, 5326),
    ["Hydra"] = CFrame.new(5229, 462, 342),
    ["GreatTree"] = CFrame.new(2091, 1497, -7130),
    ["Turtle"] = CFrame.new(-12298, 390, -7536),
}

function BF.MobFarm(on)
    _G.NW_MF = on
    if not on then
        NT("MobFarm", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_MF and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                
                local closest, dist = nil, math.huge
                for _, m in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if m:IsA("Model") and m:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        if m.Humanoid.Health > 0 and not Plrs:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m) then
                            local d = Dst(S.R.Position, m.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
                            if d < dist then
                                closest = m
                                dist = d
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                
                if closest and closest:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    S.R.CFrame = closest.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
                    VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                end
            end)
            SW(0.1)
        end
    end)
    NT("MobFarm", "ON", 2)
end

function BF.FruitESP(on)
    _G.NW_FrE = on
    if not on then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            pcall(function()
                if o.Name == "NW_FrE" then o:Destroy() end
            end)
        end
        NT("FruitESP", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_FrE and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if o:IsA("Model") and o.Name:find("Fruit") then
                        if not o:FindFirstChild("NW_FrE") then
                            CreateHighlight(o, "NW_FrE", Color3.new(1, 0, 1))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("FruitESP", "ON", 2)
end

function BF.PlayerESP(on)
    _G.NW_BFPlr = on
    if not on then
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do pcall(function() if p.Character then local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_BFP") if h then h:Destroy() end end end) end
        NT("PlayerESP", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BFPlr and S.Run do
            for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                pcall(function()
                    if p ~= P and p.Character and not p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_BFP") then
                        CreateHighlight(p.Character, "NW_BFP", Color3.new(1, 0, 0))
                    end
                end)
            end
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("PlayerESP", "ON", 2)
end

                        end
                    end
                end

            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("MobESP", "ON", 2)
end

    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BFAtk and S.Run do
            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
            task.wait(0.03)

            SW(0.05)
        end
    end)

function BF.AutoSkill(on)
    _G.NW_BFSkill = on
    if not on then NT("AutoSkill", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        local keys = {Enum.KeyCode.Z, Enum.KeyCode.X, Enum.KeyCode.C, Enum.KeyCode.V, Enum.KeyCode.F}
        local idx = 1
        while _G.NW_BFSkill and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, keys[idx], false, game)
                task.wait(0.05)
                VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, keys[idx], false, game)
                idx = idx % #keys + 1
            end)
            SW(0.3)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoSkill", "ON", 2)
end

function BF.Fly(on)
    _G.NW_BFFly = on
    if not on then
        pcall(function()
            GC()
            if S.R then
                local bv = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_BFFlyBV")
                local bg = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_BFFlyBG")
                if bv then bv:Destroy() end
                if bg then bg:Destroy() end
            end
        end)
        NT("Fly", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        GC()
        if not S.R then return end
        local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        bv.Name = "NW_BFFlyBV"
        bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bv.Parent = S.R
        local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
        bg.Name = "NW_BFFlyBG"
        bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bg.P = 10000
        bg.Parent = S.R
        while _G.NW_BFFly and S.Run do
            bv.Velocity = Cam.CFrame.LookVector * (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) and 150 or 0)
                + Cam.CFrame.RightVector * ((UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) and 150 or 0) - (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) and 150 or 0))
                + Vector3.new(0, (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) and 150 or 0) - (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) and 150 or 0), 0)
            bg.CFrame = Cam.CFrame
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("Fly", "ON", 2)
end

    _G.NW_BFNC = on

function BF.AutoCollect(on)
    _G.NW_BFCol = on
    if not on then NT("AutoCollect", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BFCol and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.R and firetouchinterest then
                    for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                        if o.Name:find("Chest") or o.Name:find("Flower") then
                            if o:IsA("BasePart") or o:IsA("Model") then
                                local part = o:IsA("BasePart") and o or o:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
                                if part then
                                    firetouchinterest(S.R, part, 0)
                                    firetouchinterest(S.R, part, 1)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoCollect", "ON", 2)
end

function BF.RaidFarm(on)
    _G.NW_BFRaid = on
    if not on then NT("RaidFarm", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BFRaid and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                for _, m in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if m:IsA("Model") and m:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        if m.Humanoid.Health > 0 and not Plrs:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m) then
                            if m.Name:find("Boss") or m.Name:find("Raid") then
                                S.R.CFrame = m.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 5)
                                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                                task.wait(0.03)
                                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.1)
        end
    end)
    NT("RaidFarm", "ON", 2)
end

function BF.Fullbright()
    pcall(function()
        Light.Ambient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        Light.Brightness = 2
        Light.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        for _, v in pairs(Light:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("Atmosphere") or v:IsA("BloomEffect") or v:IsA("BlurEffect") then
                v.Enabled = false
            end
        end
    end)
    NT("Fullbright", "ON", 2)
end

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- DOORS MODULE
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local Dr = {}
Dr.Entities = {"RushMoving", "AmbushMoving", "Screech", "Eyes", "Halt", "Seek", "Figure", "Dupe", "Glitch", "Snare", "Timothy", "A60", "A90", "A120", "Blitz", "Jeff"}

function Dr.EntityESP(on)
    _G.NW_EE = on
    if not on then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            pcall(function()
                if o.Name == "NW_EE" then o:Destroy() end
            end)
        end
        NT("EntityESP", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_EE and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    for _, e in ipairs(Dr.Entities) do
                        if o.Name == e and not o:FindFirstChild("NW_EE") then
                            CreateHighlight(o, "NW_EE", Color3.new(1, 0, 0))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.3)
        end
    end)
    NT("EntityESP", "ON", 2)
end

function Dr.DoorESP(on)
    _G.NW_DE = on
    if not on then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            pcall(function()
                if o.Name == "NW_DE" then o:Destroy() end
            end)
        end
        NT("DoorESP", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_DE and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                local rooms = WS:FindFirstChild("CurrentRooms")
                if rooms then
                    for _, r in pairs(rooms:GetChildren()) do
                        local door = r:FindFirstChild("Door") or r:FindFirstChild("ExitDoor") or r:FindFirstChild("RippleExitDoor")
                        if door and not door:FindFirstChild("NW_DE") then
                            CreateHighlight(door, "NW_DE", Color3.new(0, 0, 1))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("DoorESP", "ON", 2)
end

function Dr.TPNextDoor()
    pcall(function()
        local rooms = WS:FindFirstChild("CurrentRooms")
        if rooms then
            local h = 0
            for _, r in pairs(rooms:GetChildren()) do
                local n = tonumber(r.Name) or 0
                if n > h then h = n end
            end
            local room = rooms:FindFirstChild(tostring(h))
            if room then
                local door = room:FindFirstChild("Door") or room:FindFirstChild("ExitDoor") or room:FindFirstChild("RippleExitDoor")
                if door then
                    GC()
                    if S.R then
                        S.R.CFrame = door:GetPivot()
                        NT("TP", "Door " .. h, 2)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- BEDWARS MODULE (Based on VapeV4/InfinityHub)
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local BW = {
    Settings = {
        KillAuraRange = 15,
        Reach = 18,
        AimPart = "HumanoidRootPart"
    }
}

local function GetBedwarsKit()
    local kit = nil
    pcall(function()
        local kitData = Rep:FindFirstChild("DefaultData")
        if kitData then
            kit = kitData:FindFirstChild("Kit") and kitData.Kit.Value
        end
    end)
    return kit
end

local function IsEnemy(player)
    if player == P then return false end
    local myTeam = P.Team
    local theirTeam = player.Team
    if myTeam and theirTeam then
        return myTeam ~= theirTeam
    end
    return true
end

function BW.KillAura(on)
    _G.NW_BWKa = on
    if not on then NT("KillAura", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BWKa and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                    if IsEnemy(p) and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                            if Dst(S.R.Position, p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position) < BW.Settings.KillAuraRange then
                                local sword = nil
                                for _, tool in pairs(S.C:GetChildren()) do
                                    if tool:IsA("Tool") and (tool.Name:find("Sword") or tool.Name:find("Blade")) then
                                        sword = tool
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                                pcall(function()
                                    local combat = Rep:FindFirstChild("CombatFramework")
                                    if combat and combat:FindFirstChild("Attack") then
                                        combat.Attack:FireServer(p.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
                                    end
                                end)
                                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                                task.wait(0.03)
                                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.08)
        end
    end)
    NT("KillAura", "ON", 2)
end

function BW.AimBot(on)
    _G.NW_BWAim = on
    if not on then NT("AimBot", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BWAim and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                local closest, dist = nil, math.huge
                for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                    if IsEnemy(p) and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild(BW.Settings.AimPart) then
                        local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                            local d = Dst(S.R.Position, p.Character[BW.Settings.AimPart].Position)
                            if d < dist then closest, dist = p, d end
                        end
                    end
                end
                if closest and closest.Character then
                    Cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Cam.CFrame.Position, closest.Character[BW.Settings.AimPart].Position)
                end
            end)
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("AimBot", "ON", 2)
end

function BW.Reach(on)
    _G.NW_BWReach = on
    if not on then NT("Reach", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BWReach and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                for _, tool in pairs(S.C:GetChildren()) do
                    if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                        local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                        if handle then
                            handle.Size = Vector3.new(BW.Settings.Reach, BW.Settings.Reach, BW.Settings.Reach)
                            handle.Massless = true
                            handle.Transparency = 1
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("Reach", "ON ("..BW.Settings.Reach..")", 2)
end

function BW.SetKillAuraRange(val)
    BW.Settings.KillAuraRange = val
end

function BW.SetReach(val)
    BW.Settings.Reach = val
end

function BW.BedESP(on)
    _G.NW_BedE = on
    if not on then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do pcall(function() if o.Name == "NW_BedE" then o:Destroy() end end) end
        NT("BedESP", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BedE and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if o.Name:find("Bed") and o:IsA("Model") and not o:FindFirstChild("NW_BedE") then
                        local color = Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
                        pcall(function()
                            if o:GetAttribute("Team") == P.Team.Name then
                                color = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
                            end
                        end)
                        CreateHighlight(o, "NW_BedE", color)
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("BedESP", "ON", 2)
end

function BW.PlayerESP(on)
    _G.NW_BWPlr = on
    if not on then
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
            pcall(function()
                if p.Character then
                    local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_BWP")
                    if h then h:Destroy() end
                end
            end)
        end
        NT("PlayerESP", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BWPlr and S.Run do
            for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                pcall(function()
                    if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        if not p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_BWP") then
                            local color = IsEnemy(p) and Color3.new(1, 0, 0) or Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
                            CreateHighlight(p.Character, "NW_BWP", color)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("PlayerESP", "ON", 2)
end

function BW.ItemESP(on)
    _G.NW_BWItem = on
    if not on then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do pcall(function() if o.Name == "NW_BWItem" then o:Destroy() end end) end
        NT("ItemESP", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BWItem and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if (o.Name:find("Iron") or o.Name:find("Gold") or o.Name:find("Diamond") or o.Name:find("Emerald")) and o:IsA("BasePart") then
                        if not o:FindFirstChild("NW_BWItem") then
                            local color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0)
                            if o.Name:find("Diamond") then color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 200, 255)
                            elseif o.Name:find("Emerald") then color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)
                            elseif o.Name:find("Iron") then color = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200) end
                            CreateHighlight(o, "NW_BWItem", color)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("ItemESP", "ON", 2)
end

function BW.AutoCollect(on)
    _G.NW_BWCol = on
    if not on then NT("AutoCollect", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BWCol and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.R and firetouchinterest then
                    for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                        if (o.Name:find("Iron") or o.Name:find("Gold") or o.Name:find("Diamond") or o.Name:find("Emerald")) and o:IsA("BasePart") then
                            firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 0)
                            firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 1)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.2)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoCollect", "ON", 2)
end

function BW.BedNuker(on)
    _G.NW_BWNuke = on
    if not on then NT("BedNuker", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BWNuke and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                for _, bed in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if bed.Name:find("Bed") and bed:IsA("Model") then
                        local isEnemyBed = true
                        pcall(function()
                            if bed:GetAttribute("Team") == P.Team.Name then
                                isEnemyBed = false
                            end
                        end)
                        if isEnemyBed then
                            local bedPart = bed:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
                            if bedPart and Dst(S.R.Position, bedPart.Position) < 20 then
                                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                                task.wait(0.02)
                                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.1)
        end
    end)
    NT("BedNuker", "ON", 2)
end

function BW.Fly(on)
    _G.NW_BWFly = on
    if not on then
        pcall(function()
            GC()
            if S.R then
                local bv = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_BWFlyBV")
                local bg = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_BWFlyBG")
                if bv then bv:Destroy() end
                if bg then bg:Destroy() end
            end
        end)
        NT("Fly", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        GC()
        if not S.R then return end
        local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        bv.Name = "NW_BWFlyBV"
        bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bv.Parent = S.R
        local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
        bg.Name = "NW_BWFlyBG"
        bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bg.P = 10000
        bg.Parent = S.R
        while _G.NW_BWFly and S.Run do
            bv.Velocity = Cam.CFrame.LookVector * (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) and 80 or 0)
                + Cam.CFrame.RightVector * ((UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) and 80 or 0) - (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) and 80 or 0))
                + Vector3.new(0, (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) and 80 or 0) - (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) and 80 or 0), 0)
            bg.CFrame = Cam.CFrame
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("Fly", "ON", 2)
end

function BW.AutoBridge(on)
    _G.NW_BWBridge = on
    if not on then NT("AutoBridge", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BWBridge and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.R then
                    local ray = Ray.new(S.R.Position, Vector3.new(0, -10, 0))
                    local hit = WS:FindPartOnRay(ray, S.C)
                    if not hit then
                        VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 2)
                        task.wait(0.05)
                        VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 2)
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.1)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoBridge", "ON", 2)
end

function BW.Hitbox(size)
    _G.NW_BWHitbox = size or 10
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BWHitbox and _G.NW_BWHitbox > 0 and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= P and IsEnemy(p) and p.Character then
                        local hrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if hrp then
                            hrp.Size = Vector3.new(_G.NW_BWHitbox, _G.NW_BWHitbox, _G.NW_BWHitbox)
                            hrp.Transparency = 1
                            hrp.CanCollide = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("Hitbox", tostring(size), 2)
end

function BW.ResetHitbox()
    _G.NW_BWHitbox = 0
    pcall(function()
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= P and p.Character then
                local hrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrp then hrp.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1); hrp.Transparency = 1 end
            end
        end
    end)
    NT("Hitbox", "Reset", 2)
end

function BW.AntiVoid(on)
    _G.NW_BWVoid = on
    if not on then NT("AntiVoid", "OFF", 2) return end
    local savedPos = nil
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BWVoid and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.R then
                    if S.R.Position.Y > 0 then
                        savedPos = S.R.CFrame
                    elseif savedPos then
                        S.R.CFrame = savedPos
                    end
                end
            end)
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("AntiVoid", "ON", 2)
end

function BW.BringItems(on)
    _G.NW_BWBring = on
    if not on then NT("BringItems", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BWBring and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.R then
                    for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                        if (o.Name:find("Iron") or o.Name:find("Gold") or o.Name:find("Diamond") or o.Name:find("Emerald")) and o:IsA("BasePart") then
                            o.CFrame = S.R.CFrame
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("BringItems", "ON", 2)
end

function BW.AutoEquip(on)
    _G.NW_BWEquip = on
    if not on then NT("AutoEquip", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BWEquip and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                local best = nil
                for _, tool in pairs(P.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
                    if tool:IsA("Tool") and (tool.Name:find("Sword") or tool.Name:find("Blade")) then
                        best = tool
                    end
                end
                if best then
                    S.H:EquipTool(best)
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoEquip", "ON", 2)
end

function BW.TPBed(team)
    pcall(function()
        for _, bed in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if bed.Name:find("Bed") and bed:IsA("Model") then
                local bedTeam = bed:GetAttribute("Team")
                if bedTeam == team then
                    local bedPart = bed:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
                    if bedPart then
                        GC()
                        if S.R then
                            S.R.CFrame = bedPart.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
                            NT("TP", team .. " Bed", 2)
                        end
                    end
                    return
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end

        Cam.CameraSubject = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

    end

    NT("Spectate", "Self", 2)

                            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)

                            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                        end

                end

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- DA HOOD MODULE (30+ Functions)
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local DH = {
    Settings = {
        Prediction = 0.165,
        AimPart = "HumanoidRootPart",
        SilentAimEnabled = false,
        AimLockEnabled = false,
        LockTarget = nil,
        FOV = 150,
        ShowFOV = false
    },
    FOVCircle = nil
}

local function GetClosestPlayerDH()
    local closest, dist = nil, DH.Settings.FOV
    GC()
    if not S.R then return nil end
    for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= P and p.Character then
            local part = p.Character:FindFirstChild(DH.Settings.AimPart)
            if part then
                local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    local screenPos, onScreen = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                    if onScreen then
                        local mouse = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
                        local d = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - mouse).Magnitude
                        if d < dist then
                            closest = p
                            dist = d
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest
end

local function ApplyPrediction(target)
    if not target or not target.Character then return nil end
    local part = target.Character:FindFirstChild(DH.Settings.AimPart)
    if not part then return nil end
    local velocity = part.AssemblyLinearVelocity or part.Velocity or Vector3.zero
    return part.CFrame + (velocity * DH.Settings.Prediction)
end

function DH.CreateFOVCircle()
    if DH.FOVCircle then pcall(function() DH.FOVCircle:Remove() end) end
    pcall(function()
        DH.FOVCircle = Drawing.new("Circle")
        DH.FOVCircle.Thickness = 1
        DH.FOVCircle.Radius = DH.Settings.FOV
        DH.FOVCircle.Filled = false
        DH.FOVCircle.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
        DH.FOVCircle.Visible = DH.Settings.ShowFOV
        DH.FOVCircle.Transparency = 1
    end)
end

function DH.UpdateFOV()
    if DH.FOVCircle then
        pcall(function()
            local mouse = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
            DH.FOVCircle.Position = mouse
            DH.FOVCircle.Radius = DH.Settings.FOV
            DH.FOVCircle.Visible = DH.Settings.ShowFOV and _G.NW_DHSilent
        end)
    end
end

function DH.SilentAim(on)
    _G.NW_DHSilent = on
    DH.Settings.SilentAimEnabled = on
    
    if on then
        DH.CreateFOVCircle()
        task.spawn(function()
            while _G.NW_DHSilent and S.Run do
                DH.UpdateFOV()
                RS.RenderStepped:Wait()
            end
            if DH.FOVCircle then
                pcall(function() DH.FOVCircle.Visible = false end)
            end
        end)
        
        if not _G.NW_DHHooked then
            _G.NW_DHHooked = true
            local oldIndex
            oldIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", function(t, k)
                if _G.NW_DHSilent and (k == "Hit" or k == "Target") and t:IsA("Mouse") then
                    local target = GetClosestPlayerDH()
                    if target then
                        local predicted = ApplyPrediction(target)
                        if predicted then
                            if k == "Hit" then
                                return predicted
                            else
                                return target.Character:FindFirstChild(DH.Settings.AimPart)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return oldIndex(t, k)
            end)
        end
    end
    NT("SilentAim", on and "ON (Prediction: "..DH.Settings.Prediction.."s)" or "OFF", 2)
end

function DH.AimLock(on)
    _G.NW_DHLock = on
    if not on then
        DH.Settings.LockTarget = nil
        NT("AimLock", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_DHLock and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                if not DH.Settings.LockTarget or not DH.Settings.LockTarget.Character then
                    DH.Settings.LockTarget = GetClosestPlayerDH()
                end
                
                if DH.Settings.LockTarget and DH.Settings.LockTarget.Character then
                    local predicted = ApplyPrediction(DH.Settings.LockTarget)
                    if predicted then
                        local pos = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(predicted.Position)
                        mousemoveabs(pos.X, pos.Y)
                    end
                end
            end)
            RS.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("AimLock", "ON", 2)
end

function DH.SetPrediction(val)
    DH.Settings.Prediction = val
    NT("Prediction", tostring(val).."s", 2)
end

function DH.SetFOV(val)
    DH.Settings.FOV = val
    if DH.FOVCircle then
        pcall(function() DH.FOVCircle.Radius = val end)
    end
end

function DH.ToggleFOVCircle(on)
    DH.Settings.ShowFOV = on
    if DH.FOVCircle then
        pcall(function() DH.FOVCircle.Visible = on end)
    end
end

function DH.KillAura(on)
    _G.NW_DHKa = on
    if not on then NT("KillAura", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_DHKa and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                            if Dst(S.R.Position, p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 12 then
                                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                                task.wait(0.03)
                                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.05)
        end
    end)
    NT("KillAura", "ON", 2)
end

function DH.AutoStomp(on)
    _G.NW_DHStomp = on
    if not on then NT("AutoStomp", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_DHStomp and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= P and p.Character then
                        local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        local hrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if hum and hrp and hum.Health > 0 and hum.Health < 30 then
                            GC()
                            if S.R then
                                S.R.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
                                local args = {[1] = p}
                                pcall(function()
                                    Rep:FindFirstChild("MainEvent"):FireServer("Stomp", unpack(args))
                                end)
                                VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                                task.wait(0.05)
                                VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.15)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoStomp", "ON", 2)
end

function DH.ESP(on)
    _G.NW_DHESP = on
    if not on then
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
            pcall(function()
                if p.Character then
                    local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_DHE")
                    if h then h:Destroy() end
                end
            end)
        end
        NT("ESP", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_DHESP and S.Run do
            for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                pcall(function()
                    if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        if not p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_DHE") then
                            local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            local color = Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
                            if hum and hum.Health < 30 then
                                color = Color3.new(1, 1, 0) -- Low HP = Yellow
                            end
                            CreateHighlight(p.Character, "NW_DHE", color)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("ESP", "ON", 2)
end

function DH.TriggerBot(on)
    _G.NW_DHTrigger = on
    if not on then NT("TriggerBot", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_DHTrigger and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                local mouse = P:GetMouse()
                if mouse.Target then
                    local char = mouse.Target:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
                    if char and Plrs:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char) and Plrs:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char) ~= P then
                        VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                        task.wait(0.02)
                        VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.05)
        end
    end)
    NT("TriggerBot", "ON", 2)
end

function DH.InfiniteAmmo(on)
    _G.NW_DHAmmo = on
    if not on then NT("InfAmmo", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_DHAmmo and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, tool in pairs(P.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
                    if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                        local ammo = tool:FindFirstChild("Ammo") or tool:FindFirstChild("CurrentAmmo")
                        if ammo and ammo:IsA("NumberValue") then ammo.Value = 999 end
                    end
                end
                GC()
                if S.C then
                    for _, tool in pairs(S.C:GetChildren()) do
                        if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                            local ammo = tool:FindFirstChild("Ammo") or tool:FindFirstChild("CurrentAmmo")
                            if ammo and ammo:IsA("NumberValue") then ammo.Value = 999 end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("InfAmmo", "ON", 2)
end

function DH.GunMods(on)
    _G.NW_DHGunMod = on
    if not on then NT("GunMods", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_DHGunMod and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                local tools = {}
                if P.Backpack then for _, t in pairs(P.Backpack:GetChildren()) do if t:IsA("Tool") then table.insert(tools, t) end end end
                if S.C then for _, t in pairs(S.C:GetChildren()) do if t:IsA("Tool") then table.insert(tools, t) end end end
                for _, tool in pairs(tools) do
                    pcall(function()
                        if tool:FindFirstChild("Damage") then tool.Damage.Value = 100 end
                        if tool:FindFirstChild("Range") then tool.Range.Value = 1000 end
                        if tool:FindFirstChild("FireRate") then tool.FireRate.Value = 0.05 end
                        if tool:FindFirstChild("ReloadTime") then tool.ReloadTime.Value = 0 end
                        if tool:FindFirstChild("Spread") then tool.Spread.Value = 0 end
                        if tool:FindFirstChild("Recoil") then tool.Recoil.Value = 0 end
                    end)
                end
            end)
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("GunMods", "ON", 2)
end

function DH.Hitbox(size)
    _G.NW_DHHitbox = size or 15
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_DHHitbox and _G.NW_DHHitbox > 0 and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= P and p.Character then
                        local hrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        local head = p.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                        if hrp then
                            hrp.Size = Vector3.new(_G.NW_DHHitbox, _G.NW_DHHitbox, _G.NW_DHHitbox)
                            hrp.Transparency = 1
                            hrp.CanCollide = false
                        end
                        if head then
                            head.Size = Vector3.new(_G.NW_DHHitbox, _G.NW_DHHitbox, _G.NW_DHHitbox)
                            head.Transparency = 1
                            head.CanCollide = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("Hitbox", tostring(size), 2)
end

function DH.ResetHitbox()
    _G.NW_DHHitbox = 0
    pcall(function()
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= P and p.Character then
                local hrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local head = p.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if hrp then hrp.Size = Vector3.new(2, 2, 1); hrp.Transparency = 1 end
                if head then head.Size = Vector3.new(2, 1, 1); head.Transparency = 0 end
            end
        end
    end)
    NT("Hitbox", "Reset", 2)
end

function DH.CashESP(on)

        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do pcall(function() if o.Name == "NW_DHCash" then o:Destroy() end end) end
        NT("CashESP", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_DHCash and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if (o.Name:find("Drop") or o.Name:find("Cash") or o.Name:find("Money")) and o:IsA("BasePart") then
                        if not o:FindFirstChild("NW_DHCash") then
                            CreateHighlight(o, "NW_DHCash", Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("CashESP", "ON", 2)
end

                        if (o.Name:find("Drop") or o.Name:find("Cash") or o:IsA("Tool")) and o:IsA("BasePart") then
                            if Dst(S.R.Position, o.Position) < 50 then
                                firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 0)
                                firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 1)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.3)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoPickup", "ON", 2)

    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_DHBring and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.R then
                    for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                        if (o.Name:find("Drop") or o.Name:find("Cash") or o.Name:find("Money")) and o:IsA("BasePart") then
                            o.CFrame = S.R.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -2, 0)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("BringCash", "ON", 2)
end

function DH.TPBehind(target)
    if not target then
        local closest, dist = nil, math.huge
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                GC()
                if S.R then
                    local d = Dst(S.R.Position, p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
                    if d < dist then closest, dist = p, d end
                end
            end
        end
        target = closest
    end
    if target and target.Character and target.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        GC()
        if S.R then
            S.R.CFrame = target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
            NT("TP", "Behind " .. target.Name, 2)
        end
    end
end

function DH.LoopTPBehind(on)
    _G.NW_DHLoopTP = on
    if not on then NT("LoopTPBehind", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_DHLoopTP and S.Run do
            DH.TPBehind(DH.Settings.LockTarget)
            SW(0.1)
        end
    end)
    NT("LoopTPBehind", "ON", 2)
end

                end
            end)
        end
        Plrs.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
            p.Chatted:Connect(function(msg)
                if _G.NW_DHChat then

                end
            end)
        end)
    end)
    NT("ChatSpy", "ON (Check Console)", 2)

        end

                        for _, seat in pairs(vehicle:GetDescendants()) do
                            if seat:IsA("VehicleSeat") or seat:IsA("Seat") then
                                local occupant = seat.Occupant
                                if occupant and occupant ~= S.H then
                                    seat:SetNetworkOwner(P)
                                    occupant.Sit = false
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("Eject", "ON", 2)
