-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- BLADE BALL MODULE (25+ Functions)
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local BB = {
    Settings = {
        ParryDistance = 15,
        ParryMode = "Velocity", -- "Distance" or "Velocity"
        AutoWin = false
    }
}

local function GetBall()
    for _, obj in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj.Name == "Ball" and obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            return obj
        end
    end
    local ballsFolder = WS:FindFirstChild("Balls")
    if ballsFolder then
        for _, ball in pairs(ballsFolder:GetChildren()) do
            if ball:IsA("BasePart") or ball:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart") then
                return ball:IsA("BasePart") and ball or ball:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function GetBallTarget(ball)
    if not ball then return nil end
    local target = ball:GetAttribute("target") or ball:GetAttribute("Target")
    if target then return Plrs:FindFirstChild(target) end
    local targetVal = ball:FindFirstChild("target") or ball:FindFirstChild("Target")
    if targetVal and targetVal:IsA("ObjectValue") then
        return targetVal.Value
    end
    return nil
end

local function IsBallComingToMe(ball)
    if not ball then return false end
    GC()
    if not S.R then return false end
    
    local target = GetBallTarget(ball)
    if target == P then return true end
    
    local velocity = ball.AssemblyLinearVelocity or ball.Velocity
    if not velocity or velocity.Magnitude < 1 then return false end
    
    local toPlayer = (S.R.Position - ball.Position).Unit
    local ballDir = velocity.Unit
    local dot = toPlayer:Dot(ballDir)
    
    return dot > 0.7
end

local function CalculateParryTime(ball)
    if not ball then return math.huge end
    GC()
    if not S.R then return math.huge end
    
    local dist = Dst(ball.Position, S.R.Position)
    local velocity = ball.AssemblyLinearVelocity or ball.Velocity
    local speed = velocity and velocity.Magnitude or 50
    
    return dist / math.max(speed, 1)
end

function BB.AutoParry(on)
    _G.NW_BBParry = on
    if not on then NT("AutoParry", "OFF", 2) return end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BBParry and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                
                local ball = GetBall()
                if not ball then return end
                
                local dist = Dst(S.R.Position, ball.Position)
                local shouldParry = false
                
                if BB.Settings.ParryMode == "Velocity" then
                    if IsBallComingToMe(ball) then
                        local timeToHit = CalculateParryTime(ball)
                        if timeToHit < 0.4 and dist < 25 then
                            shouldParry = true
                        end
                    end
                else
                    if dist < BB.Settings.ParryDistance then
                        shouldParry = true
                    end
                end
                
                if shouldParry then
                    pcall(function()
                        local parryRemote = Rep:FindFirstChild("Remotes") and Rep.Remotes:FindFirstChild("ParryButtonPress")
                        if parryRemote then
                            parryRemote:FireServer()
                        end
                    end)
                    VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                    task.wait(0.02)
                    VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                end
            end)
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoParry", "ON ("..BB.Settings.ParryMode..")", 2)
end

function BB.SetParryMode(mode)
    BB.Settings.ParryMode = mode
    NT("ParryMode", mode, 2)
end

function BB.SetParryDistance(dist)
    BB.Settings.ParryDistance = dist
    NT("ParryDist", tostring(dist), 2)
end

function BB.SpamParry(on)
    _G.NW_BBSpam = on
    if not on then NT("SpamParry", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BBSpam and S.Run do
            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
            task.wait(0.02)
            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
            SW(0.05)
        end
    end)
    NT("SpamParry", "ON", 2)
end

function BB.BallESP(on)
    _G.NW_BallE = on
    if not on then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do pcall(function() if o.Name == "NW_BallE" then o:Destroy() end end) end
        NT("BallESP", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BallE and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                local ball = GetBall()
                if ball and not ball:FindFirstChild("NW_BallE") then
                    CreateHighlight(ball, "NW_BallE", Color3.new(1, 0, 0))
                end
            end)
            SW(0.3)
        end
    end)
    NT("BallESP", "ON", 2)
end

function BB.TargetESP(on)
    _G.NW_BBTarget = on
    if not on then
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
            pcall(function()
                if p.Character then
                    local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_BBT")
                    if h then h:Destroy() end
                end
            end)
        end
        NT("TargetESP", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BBTarget and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                local ball = GetBall()
                local target = ball and GetBallTarget(ball)
                for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= P and p.Character then
                        local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_BBT")
                        if h then h:Destroy() end
                        if target and p == target then
                            CreateHighlight(p.Character, "NW_BBT", Color3.new(1, 0.5, 0))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.2)
        end
    end)
    NT("TargetESP", "ON", 2)
end

function BB.PlayerESP(on)
    _G.NW_BBPlr = on
    if not on then
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do pcall(function() if p.Character then local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_BBP") if h then h:Destroy() end end end) end
        NT("PlayerESP", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BBPlr and S.Run do
            for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                pcall(function()
                    if p ~= P and p.Character and not p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_BBP") then
                        CreateHighlight(p.Character, "NW_BBP", Color3.new(1, 0, 0))
                    end
                end)
            end
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("PlayerESP", "ON", 2)
end

function BB.AutoWin(on)
    _G.NW_BBWin = on
    if not on then NT("AutoWin", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BBWin and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                local ball = GetBall()
                if ball then
                    local target = GetBallTarget(ball)
                    if target and target ~= P and target.Character then
                        GC()
                        if S.R then
                            S.R.CFrame = target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -5)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.1)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoWin", "ON", 2)
end

function BB.BallTracer(on)
    _G.NW_BBTrace = on
    NT("BallTracer", on and "ON" or "OFF", 2)
end

function BB.AutoDodge(on)
    _G.NW_BBDodge = on
    if not on then NT("AutoDodge", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BBDodge and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                local ball = GetBall()
                if ball and IsBallComingToMe(ball) then
                    GC()
                    if S.R then
                        local dodge = CFrame.new(math.random(-10, 10), 0, math.random(-10, 10))
                        S.R.CFrame = S.R.CFrame * dodge
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.3)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoDodge", "ON", 2)
end

function BB.InfJump(on)
    _G.NW_BBJump = on
    if not on then NT("InfJump", "OFF", 2) return end
    pcall(function()
        UIS.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
            if _G.NW_BBJump then
                GC()
                if S.H then S.H:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end
            end
        end)
    end)
    NT("InfJump", "ON", 2)
end

function BB.Noclip(on)
    _G.NW_BBNC = on
    if not on then NT("Noclip", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BBNC and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.C then
                    for _, p in pairs(S.C:GetDescendants()) do
                        if p:IsA("BasePart") then p.CanCollide = false end
                    end
                end
            end)
            RS.Stepped:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("Noclip", "ON", 2)
end

function BB.Fly(on)
    _G.NW_BBFly = on
    if not on then
        pcall(function()
            GC()
            if S.R then
                local bv = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_FlyBV")
                local bg = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_FlyBG")
                if bv then bv:Destroy() end
                if bg then bg:Destroy() end
            end
        end)
        NT("Fly", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        GC()
        if not S.R then return end
        local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        bv.Name = "NW_FlyBV"
        bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        bv.Parent = S.R
        local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
        bg.Name = "NW_FlyBG"
        bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bg.P = 10000
        bg.Parent = S.R
        while _G.NW_BBFly and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                bv.Velocity = Cam.CFrame.LookVector * (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) and 100 or 0)
                    + Cam.CFrame.RightVector * ((UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) and 100 or 0) - (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) and 100 or 0))
                    + Vector3.new(0, (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) and 100 or 0) - (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) and 100 or 0), 0)
                bg.CFrame = Cam.CFrame
            end)
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("Fly", "ON", 2)
end

function BB.AutoAbility(on)
    _G.NW_BBAbility = on
    if not on then NT("AutoAbility", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BBAbility and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                task.wait(0.05)
                VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
            end)
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoAbility", "ON", 2)
end

function BB.ParryBot(on)
    _G.NW_BBBot = on
    if not on then NT("ParryBot", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BBBot and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                local ball = GetBall()
                if ball then
                    local target = GetBallTarget(ball)
                    if target == P then
                        GC()
                        if S.R then
                            local dist = Dst(S.R.Position, ball.Position)
                            if dist < 20 then
                                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                                task.wait(0.02)
                                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("ParryBot", "ON", 2)
end

function BB.AntiAFK(on)
    _G.NW_BBAFK = on
    if not on then NT("AntiAFK", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BBAFK and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                local vu = game:GetService("VirtualUser")

                vu:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
            end)
            SW(60)
        end
    end)
    NT("AntiAFK", "ON", 2)
end

function BB.Hitbox(size)
    _G.NW_BBHitbox = size or 10
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BBHitbox and _G.NW_BBHitbox > 0 and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= P and p.Character then
                        local hrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if hrp then
                            hrp.Size = Vector3.new(_G.NW_BBHitbox, _G.NW_BBHitbox, _G.NW_BBHitbox)
                            hrp.Transparency = 1
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("Hitbox", tostring(size), 2)
end

function BB.Spectate(player)
    if player and player.Character then
        Cam.CameraSubject = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        NT("Spectate", player.Name, 2)
    end
end

function BB.Unspectate()
    GC()
    if S.H then Cam.CameraSubject = S.H end
    NT("Spectate", "Self", 2)
end

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- ARSENAL MODULE (25+ Functions)
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local Ars = {
    Settings = {
        AimPart = "Head",
        FOV = 200,
        Smoothness = 0.5,
        TeamCheck = true
    },
    FOVCircle = nil
}

local function GetClosestArsenalPlayer()
    local closest, dist = nil, Ars.Settings.FOV
    GC()
    if not S.R then return nil end
    for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= P and p.Character then
            if Ars.Settings.TeamCheck and p.Team == P.Team then continue end
            local part = p.Character:FindFirstChild(Ars.Settings.AimPart)
            if part then
                local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                    local screenPos, onScreen = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(part.Position)
                    if onScreen then
                        local mouse = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
                        local d = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - mouse).Magnitude
                        if d < dist then
                            closest = p
                            dist = d
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return closest
end

function Ars.AimBot(on)
    _G.NW_ArsAim = on
    if not on then NT("AimBot", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_ArsAim and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                local target = GetClosestArsenalPlayer()
                if target and target.Character then
                    local part = target.Character:FindFirstChild(Ars.Settings.AimPart)
                    if part then
                        local targetCF = CFrame.lookAt(Cam.CFrame.Position, part.Position)
                        Cam.CFrame = Cam.CFrame:Lerp(targetCF, Ars.Settings.Smoothness)
                    end
                end
            end)
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("AimBot", "ON", 2)
end

function Ars.SilentAim(on)
    _G.NW_ArsSilent = on
    if on and not _G.NW_ArsHooked then
        _G.NW_ArsHooked = true
        local oldIndex
        oldIndex = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", function(t, k)
            if _G.NW_ArsSilent and (k == "Hit" or k == "Target") and t:IsA("Mouse") then
                local target = GetClosestArsenalPlayer()
                if target and target.Character then
                    local part = target.Character:FindFirstChild(Ars.Settings.AimPart)
                    if part then
                        if k == "Hit" then
                            return part.CFrame
                        else
                            return part
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return oldIndex(t, k)
        end)
    end
    NT("SilentAim", on and "ON" or "OFF", 2)
end

function Ars.AutoFire(on)
    _G.NW_ArsFire = on
    if not on then NT("AutoFire", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_ArsFire and S.Run do
            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
            task.wait(0.03)
            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
            SW(0.08)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoFire", "ON", 2)
end

function Ars.TriggerBot(on)
    _G.NW_ArsTrig = on
    if not on then NT("TriggerBot", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_ArsTrig and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                local mouse = P:GetMouse()
                if mouse.Target then
                    local char = mouse.Target:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
                    local plr = char and Plrs:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char)
                    if plr and plr ~= P then
                        if not Ars.Settings.TeamCheck or plr.Team ~= P.Team then
                            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                            task.wait(0.02)
                            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.05)
        end
    end)
    NT("TriggerBot", "ON", 2)
end

function Ars.ESP(on)
    _G.NW_ArsESP = on
    if not on then
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
            pcall(function()
                if p.Character then
                    local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_ArsE")
                    if h then h:Destroy() end
                end
            end)
        end
        NT("ESP", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_ArsESP and S.Run do
            for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                pcall(function()
                    if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        if not p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_ArsE") then
                            local color = (p.Team == P.Team) and Color3.new(0, 1, 0) or Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
                            CreateHighlight(p.Character, "NW_ArsE", color)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("ESP", "ON", 2)
end

                GC()
                if S.C then
                    for _, tool in pairs(S.C:GetChildren()) do
                        if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                            local ammo = tool:FindFirstChild("Ammo")
                            if ammo then ammo.Value = 999 end

            SW(0.5)
        end

function Ars.KillAll(on)

    if not on then NT("KillAll", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_ArsKill and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        if not Ars.Settings.TeamCheck or p.Team ~= P.Team then
                            S.R.CFrame = p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 3)
                            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                            task.wait(0.02)
                            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                            task.wait(0.1)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("KillAll", "ON", 2)
end

function Ars.Hitbox(size)

    task.spawn(function()

function Ars.ResetHitbox()

    pcall(function()
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= P and p.Character then
                local head = p.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if head then head.Size = Vector3.new(2, 1, 1); head.Transparency = 0 end
            end
        end
    end)
    NT("Hitbox", "Reset", 2)
end

function Ars.FOVCircle(on)
    _G.NW_ArsFOVC = on
    if not on then
        if Ars.FOVCircle then pcall(function() Ars.FOVCircle.Visible = false end) end
        NT("FOVCircle", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    pcall(function()
        if not Ars.FOVCircle then
            Ars.FOVCircle = Drawing.new("Circle")
            Ars.FOVCircle.Thickness = 1
            Ars.FOVCircle.Radius = Ars.Settings.FOV
            Ars.FOVCircle.Filled = false
            Ars.FOVCircle.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
            Ars.FOVCircle.Transparency = 1
        end
        Ars.FOVCircle.Visible = true
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_ArsFOVC and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                if Ars.FOVCircle then
                    Ars.FOVCircle.Position = UIS:GetMouseLocation()
                    Ars.FOVCircle.Radius = Ars.Settings.FOV
                end
            end)
            RS.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("FOVCircle", "ON", 2)
end

-- FLEE THE FACILITY MODULE (20+ Functions)
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local FTF = {
    Settings = {
        Beast = {Enabled = false, Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50), Contrast = true},
        Survivor = {Enabled = false, Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 255, 50)},
        Computer = {Enabled = false, Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)},
        Pod = {Enabled = false, Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)},
        Exit = {Enabled = false, Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 128, 0)},
        AutoHack = false,
        AutoSave = false,
        AutoRope = false,
        AutoFreeze = false,
        SilentSwing = false,
        NoFail = false,
        Speed = 16,
        Jump = 50,
        Fullbright = false
    }
}

-- ThemeManager for smooth transitions
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local ThemeManager = {}
function ThemeManager.LerpColor(instance, property, targetColor, duration)
    if not instance then return end
    local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(duration or 0.5, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    local tween = TweenService:Create(instance, tweenInfo, {[property] = targetColor})
    tween:Play()
end

-- Helper for ESP
local function CreateFTFESP(model, name, color, text, highContrast)
    if not model then return end
    
    -- Frustum Culling Check
    local pos = model:GetPivot().Position
    local _, onScreen = Cam:WorldToViewportPoint(pos)
    if not onScreen then 
        -- If off-screen, remove ESP to save FPS
        if model:FindFirstChild("NW_ESP_Highlight") then model.NW_ESP_Highlight:Destroy() end
        if model:FindFirstChild("NW_ESP_Text") then model.NW_ESP_Text:Destroy() end
        return 
    end
    
    local h = model:FindFirstChild("NW_ESP_Highlight")
    if not h then
        h = Instance.new("Highlight")
        h.Name = "NW_ESP_Highlight"
        h.Parent = model
    end
    
    -- Update properties (allowing for dynamic updates)
    h.FillColor = color
    if highContrast then
        h.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255) -- Electric Blue for Beast
        h.OutlineTransparency = 0
        h.FillTransparency = 0.3
    else
        h.OutlineColor = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        h.OutlineTransparency = 0
        h.FillTransparency = 0.5
    end
    
    if text then
        local bg = model:FindFirstChild("NW_ESP_Text")
        if not bg then
            bg = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            bg.Name = "NW_ESP_Text"
            bg.Adornee = model
            bg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 50)
            bg.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2, 0)
            bg.AlwaysOnTop = true
            bg.Parent = model
            
            local tl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            tl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            tl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            tl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            tl.TextSize = 14
            tl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            tl.Parent = bg
        end
        
        local tl = bg:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
        if tl then
            tl.Text = text
            tl.TextColor3 = color
        end
    end
end

local function RemoveFTFESP(model)
    if not model then return end
    if model:FindFirstChild("NW_ESP_Highlight") then model.NW_ESP_Highlight:Destroy() end
    if model:FindFirstChild("NW_ESP_Text") then model.NW_ESP_Text:Destroy() end
end

function FTF.UpdateESP()
    if not S.Run then return end
    
    -- Players with State Logic
    for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
        if p ~= P and p.Character then
            local char = p.Character
            local isBeast = char:FindFirstChild("Hammer") or (p.Backpack and p.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Hammer"))
            
            if isBeast and FTF.Settings.Beast.Enabled then
                CreateFTFESP(char, "Beast", FTF.Settings.Beast.Color, "BEAST ["..p.Name.."]", true)
            elseif not isBeast and FTF.Settings.Survivor.Enabled then
                -- Survivor State Logic
                local state = "Healthy"
                local color = FTF.Settings.Survivor.Color
                local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                
                if hum then
                    if hum.Health < hum.MaxHealth * 0.4 then
                        state = "Injured"
                        color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0) -- Yellow
                    end
                    if hum.PlatformStand or char:FindFirstChild("RagdollConstraint") then -- Simple downed check
                        state = "Downed"
                        color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0) -- Red
                    end
                end
                
                CreateFTFESP(char, "Survivor", color, "Survivor ["..p.Name.."] | " .. state, false)
            else
                RemoveFTFESP(char)
            end
        end
    end
    
    -- Computers
    if FTF.Settings.Computer.Enabled then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if o.Name == "ComputerTable" then
                local status = "Computer"
                local color = FTF.Settings.Computer.Color
                local screen = o:FindFirstChild("Screen")
                if screen then
                    if screen.Color == Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0) then status = "Hacked"; color = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
                    elseif screen.Color == Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0) then status = "Error"; color = Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
                    end
                end
                CreateFTFESP(o, "Computer", color, status, false)
            end
        end
    else
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if o.Name == "ComputerTable" then RemoveFTFESP(o) end
        end
    end
    
    -- Pods
    if FTF.Settings.Pod.Enabled then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if o.Name == "FreezePod" then
                 CreateFTFESP(o, "Pod", FTF.Settings.Pod.Color, "Freeze Pod", false)
            end
        end
    else
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if o.Name == "FreezePod" then RemoveFTFESP(o) end
        end
    end
    
    -- Exits
    if FTF.Settings.Exit.Enabled then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if o.Name == "ExitDoor" then
                CreateFTFESP(o, "Exit", FTF.Settings.Exit.Color, "EXIT", false)
            end
        end
    else
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if o.Name == "ExitDoor" then RemoveFTFESP(o) end
        end
    end
end

function FTF.ToggleBeast(on) FTF.Settings.Beast.Enabled = on end
function FTF.ToggleSurvivor(on) FTF.Settings.Survivor.Enabled = on end
function FTF.ToggleComputer(on) FTF.Settings.Computer.Enabled = on end
function FTF.TogglePod(on) FTF.Settings.Pod.Enabled = on end
function FTF.ToggleExit(on) FTF.Settings.Exit.Enabled = on end

-- BEAST AUTOMATION MODULE
function FTF.AutoRope(on)
    FTF.Settings.AutoRope = on
    if on then
        task.spawn(function()
            while FTF.Settings.AutoRope and S.Run do
                pcall(function()
                    -- Check if local player is beast
                    local char = P.Character
                    if not char then return end
                    local hammer = char:FindFirstChild("Hammer") or (P.Backpack and P.Backpack:FindFirstChild("Hammer"))
                    if not hammer then return end -- Not beast
                    
                    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if not hrp then return end
                    
                    for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                        if p ~= P and p.Character then
                            local tHrp = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            local tHum = p.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                            
                            -- Check if downed/ragdolled
                            local isDowned = tHum and (tHum.PlatformStand or p.Character:FindFirstChild("RagdollConstraint"))
                            
                            if tHrp and isDowned then
                                local dist = (hrp.Position - tHrp.Position).Magnitude
                                if dist < 10 then -- Distance check (slightly increased for reliability)
                                    -- Fire rope remote
                                    local args = {
                                        [1] = "Rope",
                                        [2] = p.Character
                                    }
                                    -- Attempt to find and fire remote
                                    local remote = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("RemoteEvent") -- Example path, would need exact path
                                    if remote then remote:FireServer(unpack(args)) end
                                    
                                    -- Auto Freeze logic chaining
                                    if FTF.Settings.AutoFreeze then
                                        FTF.PathfindToPod()
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                task.wait(0.1) -- Optimized loop speed
            end
        end)
    end
end

function FTF.PathfindToPod()
    -- Simple pod finder
    local char = P.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    
    local nearestPod = nil
    local minDst = math.huge
    
    for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
        if o.Name == "FreezePod" and not o:FindFirstChild("CapturedPlayer") then
            local d = (o.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
            if d < minDst then
                minDst = d
                nearestPod = o
            end
        end
    end
    
    if nearestPod then
        -- Move to pod
        local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            hum:MoveTo(nearestPod.Position)
            -- Auto interact when close
            task.spawn(function()
                repeat 
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    if not S.Run or not FTF.Settings.AutoFreeze then return end
                until (hrp.Position - nearestPod.Position).Magnitude < 5
                
                -- Interact
                VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                task.wait()
                VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
            end)
        end
    end
end

function FTF.SilentSwing(on)
    FTF.Settings.SilentSwing = on
    -- Implementation depends on specific weapon script structure
    -- Placeholder for "No-Cooldown" logic via upvalues
    if on then
        task.spawn(function()
            while FTF.Settings.SilentSwing and S.Run do
                pcall(function()
                     local char = P.Character
                     if char then
                        local hammer = char:FindFirstChild("Hammer")
                        if hammer and hammer:FindFirstChild("LocalScript") then
                            -- debug.setupvalue logic would go here
                            -- e.g. debug.setupvalue(getsenv(hammer.LocalScript).Attack, "Cooldown", 0)
                        end
                     end
                end)
                task.wait(1)
            end
        end)
    end
end


function FTF.AutoHack(on)
    _G.NW_FTFHack = on
    if on then
        task.spawn(function()
            while _G.NW_FTFHack and S.Run do
                local char = P.Character
                if char then
                    -- Simple interaction spam (E)
                    -- Advanced: Check for nearby computers and only interact then
                     local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                     if hrp then
                        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                            if o.Name == "ComputerTable" and o:FindFirstChild("Screen") and o.Screen.Color ~= Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0) then
                                if (o.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude < 15 then
                                    VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                                    task.wait()
                                    VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                     end
                end
                task.wait(0.1)
            end
        end)
        NT("AutoHack", "ON", 2)
    else
        NT("AutoHack", "OFF", 2)
    end
end

function FTF.AutoSave(on)
    _G.NW_FTFSave = on
    if on then
        task.spawn(function()
            while _G.NW_FTFSave and S.Run do
                pcall(function()
                    local char = P.Character
                    if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                            if o.Name == "FreezePod" then
                                -- Check if someone is in it
                                local captured = o:FindFirstChild("CapturedPlayer") -- Hypothetical check
                                -- Actually usually just interacting with it works if someone is there
                                if (o.Position - char.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude < 100 then -- Only nearby
                                     if (o.Position - char.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 5 then
                                         -- Teleport to save? Maybe risky. Just interact if close.
                                     end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end)
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
        end)
    end
end

function FTF.Fullbright(on)
    FTF.Settings.Fullbright = on
    if on then
        Light.Ambient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        Light.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
        Light.Brightness = 2
    else
        Light.Ambient = Color3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5) -- Approximate default
        Light.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.new(0.5, 0.5, 0.5)
        Light.Brightness = 1
    end
end

-- Loop for ESP updates
task.spawn(function()
    while S.Run do
        pcall(FTF.UpdateESP)
        task.wait(1)
    end
end)

-- BUILD A BOAT MODULE (BABFT)

local BABFT = {}

function BABFT.AutoFarm(on)
    _G.NW_BABFarm = on
    if not on then NT("AutoFarm", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BABFarm and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.R and firetouchinterest then
                    for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                        if o.Name:find("Gold") or o.Name:find("Chest") or o.Name:find("Coin") then
                            if o:IsA("BasePart") then
                                firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 0)
                                firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 1)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoFarm", "ON", 2)
end

function BABFT.GoldESP(on)
    _G.NW_BABGold = on
    if not on then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do pcall(function() if o.Name == "NW_BABGold" then o:Destroy() end end) end
        NT("GoldESP", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BABGold and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if (o.Name:find("Gold") or o.Name:find("Chest")) and not o:FindFirstChild("NW_BABGold") then
                        CreateHighlight(o, "NW_BABGold", Color3.fromRGB(255, 215, 0))
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("GoldESP", "ON", 2)
end

local CW = {}

function CW.KillAura(on)
    _G.NW_CWKa = on
    if not on then NT("KillAura", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_CWKa and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        if Dst(S.R.Position, p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 12 then
                            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                            task.wait(0.05)
                            VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.08)
        end
    end)
    NT("KillAura", "ON", 2)
end

function CW.AutoParry(on)
    _G.NW_CWParry = on
    if not on then NT("AutoParry", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_CWParry and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        if Dst(S.R.Position, p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position) < 10 then
                            local anim = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
                            if anim then
                                for _, track in pairs(anim:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                                    if track.Name:lower():find("attack") or track.Name:lower():find("swing") then
                                        VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.F, false, game)
                                        task.wait(0.1)
                                        VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.F, false, game)
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoParry", "ON", 2)
end

function CW.AutoCombo(on)
    _G.NW_CWCombo = on
    if not on then NT("AutoCombo", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        local combo = {"M1", "M1", "M1", "M1", "M2"}
        while _G.NW_CWCombo and S.Run do
            for _, action in ipairs(combo) do
                if action == "M1" then
                    VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                elseif action == "M2" then
                    VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 2)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 2)
                end
                SW(0.15)
            end
            SW(0.3)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoCombo", "ON", 2)
end

function CW.InfStamina(on)
    _G.NW_CWStam = on
    if not on then NT("InfStamina", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_CWStam and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                if P.Character then
                    local stam = P.Character:FindFirstChild("Stamina")
                    if stam then stam.Value = 100 end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.1)
        end
    end)
    NT("InfStamina", "ON", 2)
end

function CW.AimBot(on)
    _G.NW_CWAim = on
    if not on then NT("AimBot", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_CWAim and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                local closest, dist = nil, math.huge
                for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                        local d = Dst(S.R.Position, p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
                        if d < dist then closest, dist = p, d end
                    end
                end
                if closest and closest.Character then
                    Cam.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Cam.CFrame.Position, closest.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
                end
            end)
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("AimBot", "ON", 2)
end

function PL.PlayerESP(on)
    _G.NW_PLESP = on
    if not on then
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do pcall(function() if p.Character then local h = p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_PLE") if h then h:Destroy() end end end) end
        NT("PlayerESP", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_PLESP and S.Run do
            for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
                pcall(function()
                    if p ~= P and p.Character and not p.Character:FindFirstChild("NW_PLE") then
                        local team = p.Team and p.Team.Name or "Neutral"
                        local color = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                        if team:find("Police") or team:find("Guard") then color = Color3.new(0, 0, 1)
                        elseif team:find("Criminal") then color = Color3.new(1, 0, 0)
                        elseif team:find("Prisoner") then color = Color3.new(1, 0.5, 0) end
                        CreateHighlight(p.Character, "NW_PLE", color)
                    end
                end)
            end
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("PlayerESP", "ON", 2)
end

                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("GunESP", "ON", 2)
end

function PL.Fly(on)
    _G.NW_PLFly = on
    if not on then
        pcall(function()
            GC()
            if S.R then
                local bv = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_PLFlyBV")
                local bg = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_PLFlyBG")
                if bv then bv:Destroy() end
                if bg then bg:Destroy() end
            end
        end)
        NT("Fly", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        GC()
        if not S.R then return end
        local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        bv.Name = "NW_PLFlyBV"
        bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bv.Parent = S.R
        local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
        bg.Name = "NW_PLFlyBG"
        bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bg.P = 10000
        bg.Parent = S.R
        while _G.NW_PLFly and S.Run do
            bv.Velocity = Cam.CFrame.LookVector * (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) and 100 or 0)
                + Cam.CFrame.RightVector * ((UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.D) and 100 or 0) - (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.A) and 100 or 0))
                + Vector3.new(0, (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) and 100 or 0) - (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.LeftShift) and 100 or 0), 0)
            bg.CFrame = Cam.CFrame
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("Fly", "ON", 2)
end

function PL.InfJump(on)
    _G.NW_PLJump = on
    if not on then NT("InfJump", "OFF", 2) return end
    pcall(function()

        C.TP(PL.Loc[loc])
        NT("TP", loc, 2)
    end

-- ARREST ALL (FE - Uses game remotes)
function PL.ArrestAll()
    pcall(function()
        GC()
        if not S.R then return end
        local count = 0
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                -- TP to player and arrest
                local oldPos = S.R.CFrame
                S.R.CFrame = p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, 2)
                task.wait(0.1)
                -- Fire arrest remote
                for _, r in pairs(Rep:GetDescendants()) do
                    if r:IsA("RemoteEvent") and r.Name:lower():find("arrest") then
                        pcall(function() r:FireServer(p) end)
                    end
                end
                -- Also try pressing E
                VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                task.wait(0.1)
                VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                count = count + 1
                S.R.CFrame = oldPos
                task.wait(0.2)
            end
        end
        NT("ArrestAll", count .. " players", 2)
    end)
end

-- KILL ALL (FE - Uses weapons)
function PL.KillAll()
    pcall(function()
        GC()
        if not S.R or not S.H then return end
        -- Equip weapon
        local weapon = nil
        for _, tool in pairs(P.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
            if tool:IsA("Tool") and (tool.Name:find("M9") or tool.Name:find("AK") or tool.Name:find("Shotgun") or tool.Name:find("Remington") or tool.Name:find("Knife")) then
                weapon = tool
                break
            end
        end
        if not weapon then
            for _, tool in pairs(S.C:GetChildren()) do
                if tool:IsA("Tool") then weapon = tool break end
            end
        end
        if weapon and weapon.Parent == P.Backpack then
            S.H:EquipTool(weapon)
            task.wait(0.2)
        end
        
        local count = 0
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                local oldPos = S.R.CFrame
                -- TP behind player
                S.R.CFrame = p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 0, -3)
                task.wait(0.1)
                -- Shoot/attack
                for i = 1, 10 do
                    VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                    task.wait(0.05)
                    VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                end
                count = count + 1
                S.R.CFrame = oldPos
                task.wait(0.3)
            end
        end
        NT("KillAll", count .. " players", 2)
    end)
end

-- FLING ALL (Uses velocity exploit)
function PL.FlingAll()
    pcall(function()
        GC()
        if not S.R or not S.H then return end
        
        local count = 0
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                -- Create angular velocity for fling
                local av = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
                av.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
                av.P = math.huge
                av.AngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 9999, 0)
                av.Parent = S.R
                
                -- TP to player
                S.R.CFrame = p.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                S.R.Velocity = Vector3.new(9999, 9999, 9999)
                task.wait(0.3)
                
                av:Destroy()
                count = count + 1
            end
        end
        NT("FlingAll", count .. " players", 2)
    end)
end

-- GET ALL GUNS
function PL.GetAllGuns()
    pcall(function()
        GC()
        if not S.R then return end
        local count = 0
        for _, v in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("Tool") and (v.Name:find("M9") or v.Name:find("AK") or v.Name:find("Shotgun") or v.Name:find("Remington") or v.Name:find("Knife")) then
                local handle = v:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                if handle then
                    handle.CFrame = S.R.CFrame
                    count = count + 1
                end
            end
        end
        NT("GetGuns", count .. " weapons", 2)
    end)
end

-- REMOVE ALL DOORS
function PL.RemoveDoors()
    pcall(function()
        local count = 0
        for _, v in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name:lower():find("door") then
                v:Destroy()
                count = count + 1
            end
        end
        NT("RemoveDoors", count .. " removed", 2)
    end)
end

-- TASE ALL (for police)
function PL.TaseAll()
    pcall(function()
        for _, p in pairs(Plrs:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= P and p.Character and p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                for _, r in pairs(Rep:GetDescendants()) do
                    if r:IsA("RemoteEvent") and (r.Name:lower():find("tase") or r.Name:lower():find("stun")) then
                        pcall(function() r:FireServer(p) end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        NT("TaseAll", "Done", 2)
    end)
end

local PSim = {}

function PSim.AutoFarm(on)
    _G.NW_PSFarm = on
    if not on then NT("AutoFarm", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_PSFarm and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                task.wait(0.02)
                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
            end)
            SW(0.02)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoFarm", "ON", 2)
end

function PSim.AutoHatch(on)
    _G.NW_PSHatch = on
    if not on then NT("AutoHatch", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_PSHatch and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                VIM:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                task.wait(0.1)
                VIM:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoHatch", "ON", 2)
end

function PSim.AutoCollect(on)
    _G.NW_PSCollect = on
    if not on then NT("AutoCollect", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_PSCollect and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.R and firetouchinterest then
                    for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                        if (o.Name:find("Coin") or o.Name:find("Drop") or o.Name:find("Orb")) and o:IsA("BasePart") then
                            firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 0)
                            firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 1)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.3)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoCollect", "ON", 2)
end

    _G.NW_PSSell = on

end

function PSim.PetESP(on)
    _G.NW_PSPetE = on
    if not on then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do pcall(function() if o.Name == "NW_PSPet" then o:Destroy() end end) end
        NT("PetESP", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_PSPetE and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if o:IsA("Model") and o:FindFirstChild("PetName") and not o:FindFirstChild("NW_PSPet") then
                        CreateHighlight(o, "NW_PSPet", Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 255))
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("PetESP", "ON", 2)
end

function PSim.Fly(on)
    _G.NW_PSFly = on
    if not on then
        pcall(function()
            GC()
            if S.R then
                local bv = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_PSFlyBV")
                local bg = S.R:FindFirstChild("NW_PSFlyBG")
                if bv then bv:Destroy() end
                if bg then bg:Destroy() end
            end
        end)
        NT("Fly", "OFF", 2) return
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        GC()
        if not S.R then return end
        local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        bv.Name = "NW_PSFlyBV"
        bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bv.Parent = S.R
        local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
        bg.Name = "NW_PSFlyBG"
        bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, math.huge, math.huge)
        bg.P = 10000
        bg.Parent = S.R
        while _G.NW_PSFly and S.Run do
            bv.Velocity = Cam.CFrame.LookVector * (UIS:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.W) and 100 or 0)
            bg.CFrame = Cam.CFrame
            RS.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
    end)
    NT("Fly", "ON", 2)
end

end

    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_PSBreak and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if o:IsA("Model") and (o.Name:find("Breakable") or o.Name:find("Coin") or o.Name:find("Ore")) then
                        local hrp = o:FindFirstChildOfClass("BasePart")
                        if hrp then
                            GC()
                            if S.R then
                                S.R.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
                                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                                task.wait(0.05)
                                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoBreakables", "ON", 2)
end

function LT2.BringWood(on)
    _G.NW_BW = on
    if not on then
        NT("BringWood", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BW and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                for _, log in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if log.Name == "WoodSection" or log.Name == "Log" then
                        if log:IsA("BasePart") then
                            log.CFrame = S.R.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -3, 0)
                            log.Anchored = false
                            log.CanCollide = false
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("BringWood", "ON", 2)
end

function LT2.SellWood()
    pcall(function()
        local sellCF = CFrame.new(315, 3, 85)
        for _, log in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if log.Name == "WoodSection" or log.Name == "Log" or log.Name == "Plank" then
                if log:IsA("BasePart") then
                    log.CFrame = sellCF
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    NT("Sell", "Wood sent to sell", 2)
end

function LT2.SellPlanks()
    pcall(function()
        local sellCF = CFrame.new(315, 3, 85)
        for _, plank in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if plank.Name == "Plank" then
                if plank:IsA("BasePart") then
                    plank.CFrame = sellCF
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    NT("Sell", "Planks sent to sell", 2)
end
