function LT2.PlankESP(on)
    _G.NW_PlankE = on
    if not on then
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            pcall(function()
                if o.Name == "NW_PlankE" then o:Destroy() end
            end)
        end
        NT("PlankESP", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_PlankE and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, plank in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if plank.Name == "Plank" and plank:IsA("BasePart") then
                        if not plank:FindFirstChild("NW_PlankE") then
                            CreateHighlight(plank, "NW_PlankE", Color3.fromRGB(222, 184, 135))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("PlankESP", "ON", 2)
end

function LT2.StealWood(on)
    _G.NW_Steal = on
    if not on then
        NT("StealWood", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_Steal and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R then return end
                for _, log in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if (log.Name == "WoodSection" or log.Name == "Log") and log:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local owner = log:FindFirstChild("Owner")
                        if owner and owner.Value ~= P then
                            log.CFrame = S.R.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -3, 0)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("StealWood", "ON", 2)
end

function LT2.GiftWood(target)
    if not target then
        NT("Gift", "Select a player", 2)
        return
    end
    
    pcall(function()
        local tgt = Plrs:FindFirstChild(target)
        if tgt and tgt.Character and tgt.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local targetCF = tgt.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
            for _, log in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                if (log.Name == "WoodSection" or log.Name == "Log") and log:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local owner = log:FindFirstChild("Owner")
                    if owner and owner.Value == P then
                        log.CFrame = targetCF * CFrame.new(0, -3, 0)
                    end
                end
            end
            NT("Gift", "Wood sent to " .. target, 2)
        end
    end)
end

function LT2.AutoChop(on)
    _G.NW_Chop = on
    if not on then
        NT("AutoChop", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_Chop and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R or not S.H then return end
                
                local tool = S.C:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or P.Backpack:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
                if tool and tool.Name:find("Axe") then
                    if tool.Parent == P.Backpack then
                        S.H:EquipTool(tool)
                    end
                    
                    for _, tree in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                        if tree:IsA("Model") and tree:FindFirstChild("TreeClass") then
                            local trunk = tree:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")
                            if trunk and trunk:IsA("BasePart") then
                                S.R.CFrame = trunk.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
                                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                                SW(0.3)
                                VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoChop", "ON", 2)
end

function LT2.InfMoney(on)
    _G.NW_InfM = on
    if not on then
        NT("InfMoney", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_InfM and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.R and firetouchinterest then
                    for _, drop in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                        if drop.Name == "MoneyDrop" or drop.Name:find("Money") or drop.Name:find("Cash") then
                            if drop:IsA("BasePart") then
                                firetouchinterest(S.R, drop, 0)
                                firetouchinterest(S.R, drop, 1)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.3)
        end
    end)
    NT("InfMoney", "ON", 2)
end

function LT2.DupeInfo()
    NT("Dupe", "Save, rejoin, load to dupe", 5)
end

function LT2.MaxLand()
    pcall(function()
        for _, r in pairs(Rep:GetDescendants()) do
            if r:IsA("RemoteFunction") and r.Name:find("Expand") then
                pcall(function()
                    r:InvokeServer()
                end)
            end
        end
        for _, r in pairs(Rep:GetDescendants()) do
            if r:IsA("RemoteEvent") and (r.Name:find("Land") or r.Name:find("Expand")) then
                pcall(function()
                    r:FireServer()
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
    NT("MaxLand", "Attempting...", 2)
end

function LT2.GodWood(on)
    _G.NW_GodW = on
    if not on then
        NT("GodWood", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_GodW and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, log in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if (log.Name == "WoodSection" or log.Name == "Log" or log.Name == "Plank") and log:IsA("BasePart") then
                        log.Anchored = true
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("GodWood", "ON (Anchored)", 2)
end

function LT2.TP(loc)
    if LT2.Loc[loc] then
        C.TP(LT2.Loc[loc])
        NT("TP", loc, 2)
    end
end

-- TP CHOP - Teleport to tree, chop it, return with wood (Kron/Butter style)
LT2.SavedPos = nil
function LT2.TPChop(on)
    _G.NW_TPChop = on
    if not on then NT("TPChop", "OFF", 2) return end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_TPChop and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if not S.R or not S.H then return end
                
                -- Save current position
                LT2.SavedPos = S.R.CFrame
                
                -- Find axe
                local axe = nil
                for _, tool in pairs(P.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
                    if tool:IsA("Tool") and tool.Name:find("Axe") then axe = tool break end
                end
                if not axe then
                    for _, tool in pairs(S.C:GetChildren()) do
                        if tool:IsA("Tool") and tool.Name:find("Axe") then axe = tool break end
                    end
                end
                if not axe then return end
                
                -- Equip axe
                if axe.Parent == P.Backpack then S.H:EquipTool(axe) end
                
                -- Find closest tree
                local closestTree, closestDist = nil, math.huge
                for _, tree in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if tree:IsA("Model") and tree:FindFirstChild("TreeClass") then
                        local trunk = tree:FindFirstChild("WoodSection")
                        if trunk and trunk:IsA("BasePart") then
                            local d = (trunk.Position - S.R.Position).Magnitude
                            if d < closestDist then
                                closestTree = trunk
                                closestDist = d
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                
                if closestTree then
                    -- TP to tree
                    S.R.CFrame = closestTree.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0)
                    SW(0.2)
                    
                    -- Chop (click spam)
                    for i = 1, 30 do
                        if not _G.NW_TPChop then break end
                        VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, true, game, 1)
                        task.wait(0.05)
                        VIM:SendMouseButtonEvent(0, 0, 0, false, game, 1)
                        task.wait(0.1)
                    end
                    
                    SW(0.5)
                    
                    -- Bring wood to saved position
                    for _, log in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                        if (log.Name == "WoodSection" or log.Name == "Log") and log:IsA("BasePart") then
                            local owner = log:FindFirstChild("Owner")
                            if owner and owner.Value == P then
                                log.CFrame = LT2.SavedPos * CFrame.new(0, -3, 0)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    
                    -- Return to saved position
                    S.R.CFrame = LT2.SavedPos
                end
            end)
            SW(2)
        end
    end)
    NT("TPChop", "ON - Auto farming", 2)
end

-- Free Land (uses game remotes)
function LT2.FreeLand()
    pcall(function()
        -- Find land purchase remotes
        for _, r in pairs(Rep:GetDescendants()) do
            if r:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                local n = r.Name:lower()
                if n:find("land") or n:find("claim") or n:find("buy") or n:find("purchase") then
                    pcall(function() r:FireServer() end)
                end
            end
        end
        -- Try clicking land buttons
        for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
            if o:IsA("ClickDetector") then
                local parent = o.Parent
                if parent and parent.Name:lower():find("land") then
                    if fireclickdetector then fireclickdetector(o) end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    NT("FreeLand", "Attempting...", 2)
end

-- Instant Blueprints
function LT2.InstantBlueprints(on)
    _G.NW_InstBP = on
    if not on then NT("InstantBP", "OFF", 2) return end
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_InstBP and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, bp in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    if bp.Name == "Blueprint" or bp:FindFirstChild("Blueprint") then
                        local ghost = bp:FindFirstChild("GhostPart") or bp
                        if ghost and ghost:IsA("BasePart") then
                            ghost.Transparency = 0
                            ghost.CanCollide = true
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    NT("InstantBP", "ON", 2)
end

-- Copy Base (teleports your base items to another player's base)
function LT2.CopyBase(targetPlayer)
    if not targetPlayer then NT("CopyBase", "Select player", 2) return end
    pcall(function()
        local tgt = Plrs:FindFirstChild(targetPlayer)
        if not tgt then return end
        -- Find target's base
        local targetBase = WS:FindFirstChild(tgt.Name .. "'s Base") or WS:FindFirstChild(tgt.Name)
        if targetBase then
            GC()
            if S.R then
                S.R.CFrame = targetBase:GetPivot()
                NT("CopyBase", "TP to " .. targetPlayer, 2)
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- Slot Dupe Info
function LT2.SlotDupe()
    NT("SlotDupe", "1. Fill slot 2. Rejoin 3. Load = Dupe", 5)
end

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- TYCOON & OBBY UNIVERSAL
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local Ty = {}
local Ob = {CPs = {}}

function Ty.AutoBuy(on)
    _G.NW_TB = on
    if not on then
        NT("AutoBuy", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_TB and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    local n = o.Name:lower()
                    if n:find("button") or n:find("buy") or n:find("purchase") then
                        if o:IsA("BasePart") then
                            local pp = o:FindFirstChild("ProximityPrompt")
                            if pp and fireproximityprompt then
                                fireproximityprompt(pp)
                            end
                            local cd = o:FindFirstChildOfClass("ClickDetector")
                            if cd and fireclickdetector then
                                fireclickdetector(cd)
                            end
                            GC()
                            if S.R and firetouchinterest then
                                firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 0)
                                firetouchinterest(S.R, o, 1)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.3)
        end
    end)
    NT("AutoBuy", "ON", 2)
end

function Ty.BringDrops(on)
    _G.NW_BD = on
    if not on then
        NT("BringDrops", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_BD and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                GC()
                if S.R then
                    for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                        local n = o.Name:lower()
                        if n:find("cash") or n:find("money") or n:find("coin") or n:find("drop") then
                            if o:IsA("BasePart") then
                                o.Transparency = 0.9
                                o.CanCollide = false
                                o.CFrame = S.R.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -3, 0)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.3)
        end
    end)
    NT("BringDrops", "ON", 2)
end

function Ob.FindCPs()
    Ob.CPs = {}
    for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
        local n = o.Name:lower()
        if n:find("checkpoint") or n:find("stage") or n:find("spawn") then
            if o:IsA("BasePart") or o:IsA("Model") then
                table.insert(Ob.CPs, o)
            end
        end
    end
    table.sort(Ob.CPs, function(a, b)
        return (tonumber(a.Name:match("%d+")) or 0) < (tonumber(b.Name:match("%d+")) or 0)
    end)
    return #Ob.CPs
end

function Ob.SkipEnd()
    Ob.FindCPs()
    if #Ob.CPs > 0 then
        local cp = Ob.CPs[#Ob.CPs]
        GC()
        if S.R then
            local pos = cp:IsA("Model") and cp:GetPivot().Position or cp.Position
            S.R.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0))
            NT("Skip", "End", 2)
        end
    end
end

function Ob.KillBypass(on)
    _G.NW_KB = on
    if not on then
        NT("KillBypass", "OFF", 2)
        return
    end
    
    task.spawn(function()
        while _G.NW_KB and S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                for _, o in pairs(WS:GetDescendants()) do
                    local n = o.Name:lower()
                    if n:find("kill") or n:find("lava") or n:find("death") then
                        if o:IsA("BasePart") then
                            o.CanCollide = false
                            o.Transparency = 0.8
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            SW(0.5)
        end
    end)
    NT("KillBypass", "ON", 2)
end

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- LOAD UI
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local Flu, Sav, Int

local function LoadUI()
    local s, err = pcall(function()
        -- sUNC Polyfill / Executor Compatibility
        if not getgenv then
            getgenv = function() return _G end
        end
        if not identifyexecutor then
            getgenv().identifyexecutor = function() return "Unknown" end
        end
        if not request then
             getgenv().request = http_request or (syn and syn.request) or (fluxus and fluxus.request) or function(req) return {Body = game:HttpGet(req.Url)} end
        end

        -- Load MacLib (User requested specific URL)
        local url = "https://github.com/biggaboy212/Maclib/releases/latest/download/maclib.txt"
        local content = game:HttpGet(url)
        if not content or #content == 0 then error("Failed to download MacLib") end
        
        local libFunc, loadErr = loadstring(content)
        if not libFunc then error("MacLib syntax error: " .. tostring(loadErr)) end
        
        local MacLib = libFunc()
        if not MacLib then error("MacLib returned nil") end
        
        -- Fluent Compatibility Wrapper
        Flu = {}
        function Flu:CreateWindow(opt)
            -- MacLib Window: Title, Theme, Keybind
            -- User requested "no themes", so we use a default one.
            -- Using pcall to prevent errors if args are wrong
            local win = nil
            pcall(function()
                 win = MacLib:Window(opt.Title, "Dark", opt.MinimizeKey or Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl)
            end)
            if not win then
                 -- Fallback if 3 args failed, try table (some MacLib versions vary)
                 pcall(function()
                    win = MacLib:Window({Title = opt.Title, Theme = "Dark", MinimizeKey = opt.MinimizeKey or Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl})
                 end)
            end
            if not win then
                 -- Ultimate fallback
                 win = MacLib:Window(opt.Title, Color3.new(0,1,1), Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl) 
            end
            
            local winWrapper = {}
            
            function winWrapper:AddTab(opt)
                local tab = win:Tab(opt.Title)
                local tabWrapper = {}
                
                function tabWrapper:AddSection(title)
                    tab:Section(title)
                end
                
                function tabWrapper:AddButton(opt)
                    tab:Button(opt.Title, opt.Callback)
                end
                
                function tabWrapper:AddToggle(id, opt)
                    tab:Toggle(opt.Title, opt.Default or false, opt.Callback)
                end
                
                function tabWrapper:AddSlider(id, opt)
                    tab:Slider(opt.Title, opt.Min, opt.Max, opt.Default or opt.Min, opt.Callback)
                end
                
                function tabWrapper:AddDropdown(id, opt)
                    pcall(function() tab:Dropdown(opt.Title, opt.Values, opt.Callback) end)
                end
                
                function tabWrapper:AddInput(id, opt)
                    pcall(function() tab:Textbox(opt.Title, opt.Default or "", opt.Callback) end)
                end
                
                function tabWrapper:AddParagraph(opt)
                    tab:Label(opt.Title .. ": " .. (opt.Content or ""))
                end
                
                function tabWrapper:AddColorPicker(id, opt)
                    pcall(function() tab:Colorpicker(opt.Title, opt.Default or Color3.new(1,1,1), opt.Callback) end)
                end
                
                return tabWrapper
            end
            
            function winWrapper:SelectTab(index) end
            return winWrapper
        end
        
        function Flu:SetTheme(v) end
        
        -- Mock Managers
        Sav = { SetLibrary = function() end, SetFolder = function() end, BuildConfigSection = function() end }
        Int = { SetLibrary = function() end, BuildInterfaceSection = function() end }
    end)
    
    if not s then warn("[NW] LoadUI Failed: " .. tostring(err)) end
    return s
end

-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- BUILD UI
-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
local function Build()
    local W = Flu:CreateWindow({
        Title = "NexusWare Pro v12",
        SubTitle = CG,
        TabWidth = 130,
        Size = UDim2.fromOffset(560, 460),
        Acrylic = true,
        Theme = "Dark",
        MinimizeKey = Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl
    })
    
    -- PLAYER TAB
    local PT = W:AddTab({Title = "Player", Icon = "user"})
    PT:AddSection("Movement")
    PT:AddSlider("Spd", {Title = "Speed", Default = 16, Min = 0, Max = 500, Rounding = 0, Callback = C.Spd})
    PT:AddSlider("Jmp", {Title = "Jump", Default = 50, Min = 0, Max = 500, Rounding = 0, Callback = C.Jmp})
    PT:AddSlider("FS", {Title = "FlySpeed", Default = 100, Min = 10, Max = 1000, Rounding = 0, Callback = function(v) _G.NW_FS = v end})
    PT:AddToggle("Fly", {Title = "Fly", Default = false, Callback = function(v) C.Fly(v, _G.NW_FS) end})
    PT:AddToggle("NC", {Title = "Noclip", Default = false, Callback = C.NC})
    PT:AddToggle("IJ", {Title = "InfJump", Default = false, Callback = C.IJ})
    PT:AddToggle("CTP", {Title = "Click TP", Default = false, Callback = C.ClickTP})
    PT:AddSlider("Grav", {Title = "Gravity", Default = 196, Min = 0, Max = 500, Rounding = 0, Callback = C.Gravity})
    PT:AddButton({Title = "Reset Gravity", Callback = C.ResetGravity})
    PT:AddButton({Title = "Reset Camera", Callback = C.ResetCamera})
    PT:AddButton({Title = "Cleanup Self (Fix Blocks/Light)", Callback = C.CleanupSelf})

        ABT:AddDropdown("AimPart", {Title = "Aim Part", Values = {"Head", "HumanoidRootPart", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso"}, Default = "Head", Callback = C.AimPart})
        ABT:AddSlider("AimFOV", {Title = "FOV Size", Default = 150, Min = 50, Max = 500, Rounding = 0, Callback = C.AimFOV})
        ABT:AddToggle("ShowFOV", {Title = "Show FOV Circle", Default = true, Callback = C.AimShowFOV})
        ABT:AddSlider("AimSmooth", {Title = "Smoothness (0=Instant)", Default = 0, Min = 0, Max = 10, Rounding = 1, Callback = C.AimSmoothness})
        ABT:AddSlider("AimPred", {Title = "Prediction", Default = 0, Min = 0, Max = 0.3, Rounding = 3, Callback = C.AimPrediction})
        
        ABT:AddSection("Aimbot Checks")
        ABT:AddToggle("AimTC", {Title = "Team Check", Default = true, Callback = C.AimTeamCheck})
        ABT:AddToggle("AimWC", {Title = "Wall Check", Default = false, Callback = C.AimWallCheck})
        ABT:AddToggle("AimSticky", {Title = "Sticky Aim (Lock Target)", Default = false, Callback = C.AimStickyAim})
        
        ABT:AddSection("Silent Aim")
        ABT:AddSlider("SAimFOV", {Title = "Silent FOV", Default = 150, Min = 50, Max = 500, Rounding = 0, Callback = C.SilentAimFOV})
        ABT:AddSlider("SAimPred", {Title = "Silent Prediction", Default = 0.165, Min = 0, Max = 0.3, Rounding = 3, Callback = C.SilentAimPrediction})
        ABT:AddToggle("SAimTC", {Title = "Silent Team Check", Default = true, Callback = C.SilentAimTeamCheck})
    end
    
    PT:AddSection("Visual")
    PT:AddToggle("ESP", {Title = "Player ESP (Master)", Default = false, Callback = C.ESP})
    PT:AddToggle("Invis", {Title = "Invisible", Default = false, Callback = C.InvisChar})
    PT:AddButton({Title = "Fullbright", Callback = C.FB})
    
    -- ADVANCED ESP TAB (Not useful for Fisch)
    if CG ~= "Fisch" then
        local ET = W:AddTab({Title = "ESP Pro", Icon = "eye"})
        ET:AddSection("ESP Components")
        ET:AddToggle("ESPH", {Title = "Highlights (Glow)", Default = true, Callback = C.ESPHighlights})
        ET:AddToggle("ESPB", {Title = "2D Boxes", Default = false, Callback = C.ESPBoxes})
        ET:AddToggle("ESPT", {Title = "Tracers", Default = false, Callback = C.ESPTracers})
        ET:AddToggle("ESPN", {Title = "Names", Default = true, Callback = C.ESPNames})
        ET:AddToggle("ESPHP", {Title = "Health Bars", Default = true, Callback = C.ESPHealth})
        ET:AddToggle("ESPD", {Title = "Distance", Default = true, Callback = C.ESPDistance})
        ET:AddToggle("ESPSK", {Title = "Skeleton", Default = false, Callback = C.ESPSkeleton})
        
        ET:AddSection("ESP Settings")
        ET:AddToggle("ESPTC", {Title = "Team Check", Default = true, Callback = C.ESPTeamCheck})

    ST:AddSection("Server")
    ST:AddButton({Title = "ServerHop", Callback = C.SrvHop})
    ST:AddButton({Title = "Rejoin", Callback = C.Rejoin})
    ST:AddButton({Title = "StopAll", Callback = function()
        for k in pairs(_G) do
            if type(k) == "string" and k:find("^NW_") then
                _G[k] = nil
            end
        end
        NT("Stopped", "All scripts", 2)
    end})
    
    ST:AddParagraph({Title = "NexusWare Pro v12", Content = "Game: " .. PName:sub(1, 25) .. "\nType: " .. CG .. "\nID: " .. PId})
    
    W:SelectTab(1)
    NT("NexusWare", "v12 | " .. CG, 5)
end

if LoadUI() then
    Build()
    RemoveBlur()
    CleanLocalHighlights()
    
    -- Delayed blur removal (catches Fluent UI blur)
    task.delay(0.5, RemoveBlur)
    task.delay(1, RemoveBlur)
    task.delay(2, RemoveBlur)
    
    -- Periodic cleanup for local player highlights (every 1 second)
    task.spawn(function()
        while S.Run do
            pcall(function()
                CleanLocalHighlights()
                -- MacLib does not use Lighting.Blur, so we don't need to force remove it constantly
                -- This prevents "Blur is not a valid member of Lighting" errors if user deletes it manually
            end)
            SW(1)
        end
    end)
    
    print("[NW] Loaded!")
else
    warn("[NW] UI failed to load")
end
